# M3.3 Supplements Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (chosen by Andrew on 13 Oct 2026) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The household keeps a list of supplements, starting with Vitamin D at 400 IU. A parent logs a dose of one from Today in two taps, and the phone asks before a second dose of a supplement on the same day. A dose shows on the other phone within 2 seconds. It also shows in Today's last 24 hours, on History's Day, Week and List views, in Recently deleted once deleted, and on the CSV. It can be moved, deleted and restored. Settings adds a supplement, renames it, changes its dose, or stops giving it after asking. History's Log at menu offers each supplement in use.

**Architecture:**
- **`packages/contracts`:** a new `supplements.ts` holds:
  - `Supplement` and `Dose`, and the requests that change them
  - `SupplementConflict` and `DoseConflict`
  - the `supplements` and `doses` groups

  `Me`'s household gains `supplements`. `Today` gains `doses`, and `RecentEntry` gains a dose kind. Each period and `Deleted` gain `doses`.
- **`apps/worker`:** migration `0008-supplements` adds `supplements` and `supplement_logs`, and gives a household that already exists Vitamin D. `core/supplements/` holds the definitions (`definitions.ts`), the dose reads (`doses.ts`) and the dose writes (`dose-writes.ts`). `supplement-handlers.ts` serves both groups through the receipts. Today, the periods, Recently deleted and the CSV read doses. The birth-date check counts them, and a zone change refiles them.
- **`apps/web`:**
  - The data layer sends the four dose actions and the supplement saves. It shows a dose at once on Today and every cached day: pending, taken back, saved or swapped.
  - Today gains a Supplements card, and a dose sheet that asks before a second dose.
  - The last 24 hours, the edit sheets, held entries, History's Day, Week and List views, Recently deleted and Settings › Supplements all show doses. History's Log at menu offers each supplement in use.
- **`packages/domain`:** unchanged. Doses aren't totalled (D4).

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.9.3, `effect` 4.0.1 (Schema, `effect/http-api`), `@bt/contracts` and `@bt/domain`, Workers and Durable Objects (wrangler 4.148.0, `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`), React 19.3.0, TanStack Query 5.104.1 and Router 1.170.41, Base UI 1.8.0 with shadcn, Tailwind 4.3.3, lucide-react 1.51.0 (its `PillIcon`), Vitest 4.1.11, Playwright 1.64.0, oxlint 1.87.0 and oxfmt 0.72.0, Node 24.21.0. M3.3 adds no package.

**Spec:** `docs/implementation-plan.md`:
- the M3.3 row, and these decisions:
  - D8: `local_date` is recomputed when the time zone changes
  - D10: a restore re-runs every check
  - D13: Today's prefill
- §3.1, §3.2 (the conflict codes, `SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY` among them) and §3.3 (migrations, the clock, receipts, audit)
- §7: AC 2 (two taps) and AC 6 (the two-context E2E)
- §8's row on migrations, and `CLAUDE.md`'s migration rule: before a migration merges, it's tried on a fresh export, the restore drill runs, and `pnpm bench` runs

It also builds on these plans:
- M3.1's (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-m3.1-sleep-core.md` on its branch): every place a new kind joins, and `pnpm migrations:dry-run`
- M1.8's P11: installed apps skip a `recent` kind they don't know
- M1.12 and M5.6: the sheets frame and held entries
- M3.4: edit sheets. A kept time skips the checks, and a conflict offers "Save mine anyway".
- M3.5 (Recently deleted), M3.7 and M3.8 (the Day and Week views), M3.9 (the List view, PR #70), M3.11 (the CSV) and M5.5 (the bench)

## Global Constraints

- **Where to work:** the worktree `.claude/worktrees/m3.3-supplements`, on `feature/supplements-definitions-crud-vitamin-d-seeded-logs-with-local-date-duplicate-day-409-record-another-dose-anyway-confirmed-duplicate-vitamin-d-logged-at-9-10-by-emily-on-today.M3.3`. Never the main checkout, which is for releases. A worktree-isolated Claude session runs git with plain commands from the worktree root: no `git -C`, no `cd` into another checkout, and no loops around git.
- **Node:** run commands from the worktree root with Node 24.21.0, from `.nvmrc`. In a Claude Code shell, `node` is v22. Put `/Users/andamonium/.nvm/versions/node/v24.21.0/bin` first on `PATH`. A wrapper that exports it and runs `pnpm "$@"` works in a worktree session.
- **Packages:** exact versions, each defined once in the `pnpm-workspace.yaml` catalog. M3.3 adds no dependency.
- **Imports:**
  - `packages/contracts` imports `@bt/domain` and `effect`.
  - `apps/worker` imports `@bt/contracts`, `@bt/domain`, `effect`, `jose`, `cloudflare:*` and its own files.
  - `apps/web` imports through `@bt/contracts`, `@bt/domain` and its catalog packages.
  - Lint enforces all of it.
- **Style:** no semicolons, double quotes, 2-space indent, 120 columns, and `.ts` extensions on relative imports. `pnpm format` writes and `pnpm lint` checks. The code below is already formatted.
- **Lint bans comments of every kind** in TypeScript (`gamesheet/no-comments`). Don't turn a comment into JSDoc or a `SAFETY:` comment to get past the rule. Put the reason in a name or a test title.
- **Other lint rules:**
  - No type assertions except `as const`.
  - Complexity is at most 10 per function.
  - The anti-slop rules ban `unknown` and `object` parameters, `typeof` checks, "shape" in names, and `vi.mock`.
  - `Schema.Number` is banned: use `Schema.Finite` or `Schema.Int`.
- **The web's own rules:**
  - Components only display. Their rules live in tested `.ts` files beside them, as `supplement-rows.ts` and `dose-session.ts` do.
  - No new effects.
  - Files stay near 100 lines.
  - Colours are semantic tokens, and touch targets are at least 44 px.
  - Keep M1.10's calm look: no new fonts, colours or motion.
- **The core is synchronous (D2).** Every mutation is one synchronous function inside the receipt's `transactionSync`, with no `await`. Core functions take a `Stamp` (`now` and the caregiver). Only the Durable Object's edge reads the clock.
- **Receipts:** every mutation goes through `mutations(...)`. A retry with the same `actionId` answers the stored response. One with edited values answers `ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH` with the saved record. Once this deploys, `Supplement` and `Dose` are stored responses, so a later change to either must stay decodable for 24 hours (`CLAUDE.md`).
- **Responses:** every response the Worker makes carries `cache-control: no-store`.
- **Zones in tests:**
  - The Worker fixtures' `NOW` is 8 October 2026 at noon in America/Vancouver, the seed's zone.
  - The web fixtures' `NOW` is 9 October 2026 at 9:30 AM there.
  - History's E2E specs run the browser in Europe/London while the household is in Vancouver, so a time read on the wrong clock shows.
- **E2E:** run `pnpm exec playwright test --config .superpowers/e2e.config.ts` on port 4209 (Setup Step 3). Never run `pnpm e2e` on 4173, which other sessions' builds may hold.
  - Other worktrees hold 4184 to 4189, 4191, 4192, 4194 to 4199, 4201, 4203 (M3.1), 4206 and 4208 (M3.2).
  - 4190 answers every request 500 on this machine.
  - WebKit's "reconnects after the socket drops" in `live.spec.ts` times out about one run in seven, as it has since M1.10. Compare with `main` before blaming the branch.
- **Sessions alongside, 13 Oct 2026:**
  - **fa** builds M3.1 (#43) by subagents on `feature/sleep-core-…M3.1`, which has all ten tasks at `75f3660`; its final review and PR are still ahead. This branch starts from it (Setup) and merges `main` once it lands.
  - **4e** has planned M3.2 (#44), the sleep UI, on `scratch/m3.2-replay` at `392bb81`, and opens its PR after M3.1's. Whichever of M3.2 and M3.3 merges second reconciles the places both touch (Handoffs).
  - **0e** built M3.9 (#46). Its PR #70 merged on 13 Oct 2026 as `2966d9f`. Task 12 merges it and gives the list its dose rows.
  - Tell the other sessions by `SendMessage` before changing a name, field, code or path another one reads:
    - in the contracts: `supplements.ts`, `Today`, `RecentEntry`, the period answers, `Deleted` and `Me`
    - the CSV's columns and `seedTwoYears`
    - on the web: `SHOWN_KINDS`, `SheetKind`, `TimedSheet`, `showAt`, `NEW_ENTRIES`, `TODAY_SHEETS`, `Display`, `recentRow` and the Week view's lanes
- **Household values:** the child's name and birth date, the parents' emails, and the household's name and time zone never go in issues, PRs, commits or the repo (`CLAUDE.md`). Exports hold real data. Keep them outside the checkout, or under its `exports/`, which git ignores, and never paste their contents.
- **Out of scope:**
  - deleting a supplement (P4)
  - schedules or reminders
  - counting doses anywhere (D4)
  - sleep (M3.2)

## Decisions this plan makes (veto in review)

- **P1: Migration `0008-supplements` adds two tables.**
  - `supplements` has `id` and `name` (1 to 60 characters). It also has `dose` (at most 40 characters, maybe empty) and `stopped_at` (null while it's given). Then `version`, `created_by`/`created_at` and `updated_by`/`updated_at`, with each instant checked to fall in 2000 to 2099. `created_by` and `updated_by` are null for the seeded Vitamin D.
  - `supplement_logs` holds the doses:
    - `id` and `supplement_id`, which references `supplements`
    - `occurred_at`
    - `local_date`, checked to look like a date
    - `dose`, the copy (P5)
    - `version`, the audit columns and `deleted_at`
  - Two indexes: `supplement_logs_by_date (local_date, supplement_id)` serves the same-day question, and `supplement_logs_by_time (occurred_at)` serves Today, the periods and the CSV.
  - There's no `source` or `source_ref` (D4): they served the import, which was dropped with M2.
  - It isn't a Durable Object class change, so CI deploys it.
- **P2: Every household has Vitamin D at 400 IU.**
  - 0008 gives it to a household that already exists, with a fixed id. It inserts with `SELECT … FROM household`, so an empty database gets none from the migration: a test's fresh Durable Object, or a new household before its seed.
  - `insertHousehold` gives a new household its own through `seedVitaminD`, with a random id.
  - Neither has an author.
  - `pnpm migrations:dry-run` loads the export's rows before it applies 0008, so it shows the household getting Vitamin D.
- **P3: Six endpoints in two groups, each through the receipts.**
  - `POST /api/supplements` (`addSupplement`) takes `NewSupplement`: `{ actionId, name, dose }`. It answers 201.
  - `PATCH /api/supplements/:id` (`editSupplement`) takes `SupplementEdit`: `{ actionId, version, name, dose, stopped }`.
  - `POST /api/doses` (`logDose`) takes `NewDose`: `{ actionId, supplementId, occurredAt, confirmedDuplicate }`. It answers 201.
  - `PATCH /api/doses/:id` (`editDose`) takes `DoseEdit`: `{ actionId, version, occurredAt }`.
  - `DELETE /api/doses/:id` (`deleteDose`) and `POST /api/doses/:id/restore` (`restoreDose`) take `VersionedAction`.
  - The answers are `SavedSupplement` (`{ supplement, changed }`) and `SavedDose` (`{ dose, changed }`).
  - The errors are `InvalidInput`, `NotFound` (not on `addSupplement`), `SupplementConflict` (`STALE_VERSION`, `ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH`) and `DoseConflict` (`SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY`, `STALE_VERSION`, `ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH`).
  - The checks follow the diapers'. A version that isn't current answers `STALE_VERSION` with the record as it is now, deleted or not. The right version in the wrong state answers 404: an edit to a deleted dose, or a restore of a live one.
  - A supplement's save changes `me`. A dose's save changes its days (D7), and a delete or restore also changes `deleted`.
- **P4: A supplement is saved whole, and stopped, never deleted.**
  - `stopped: true` stops it from now. A second stop keeps the first stop's time. `stopped: false` gives it again.
  - No endpoint deletes a supplement, since its doses point at it.
  - Two supplements may share a name.
  - The phone checks the fields before sending. On `STALE_VERSION` it saves once more, on top of the other phone's newer copy. It keeps the fields this parent didn't change from that copy, as M1.15's household saves do. A second `STALE_VERSION` is shown.
- **P5: A dose keeps its supplement's dose as it was when logged (D2).**
  - The Worker copies `dose` from the supplement as the household holds it at the insert, never from the phone.
  - A move changes only the time.
  - The phone's pending dose copies the dose its cached `Me` shows, until the household answers.
- **P6: The same-day question (D3).**
  - `logDose` answers `SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY` with that supplement's latest live dose on the household's date of `occurredAt`, unless `confirmedDuplicate` is true. The check runs inside the receipt's transaction. Two phones logging at once save one dose, and the other phone asks.
  - On the phone, "Record another dose" resends at the same time, with a new `actionId` and `confirmedDuplicate: true`. "Don't record it" drops the held failure and closes the sheet.
  - Moving a dose, restoring one and a zone change never ask, since none of them gives a dose.
  - A dose of a stopped supplement is still saved. The phones stop offering it, but a dose sent by a phone that hadn't heard is kept, not refused.
- **P7: `local_date` is the household's date at `occurred_at` (D8).**
  - It's set when a dose is logged and recomputed when one moves.
  - A zone change recomputes it for every dose, deleted ones included, in its own save (`refileDoses` in `editHousehold`). That doesn't bump a version, since the date is derived.
  - `local_date` drives only the same-day question. Today and the periods select doses by `occurred_at`.
- **P8: A dose's time is checked as every entry's is.**
  - `timeIssue` refuses a time more than 5 minutes ahead (`in_future`) or before the birth date (`before_birth`).
  - A move that keeps the time skips both checks (M3.4's rule).
  - A restore re-runs them (D10), but not the same-day question.
  - The birth date can't move past a live dose (`earliestRecordAt`).
- **P9: Doses are listed, never totalled (D4).**
  - `Totals`, `hasEntries` and Trends ignore them.
  - Today answers `doses`: today's live doses, oldest first. Today's `recent` gains `{ kind: "dose", dose }` for every live dose of the last 24 hours, newest first among the feeds and diapers.
  - Each period answers `doses`: the live doses in its bounds, by time.
  - `Deleted` answers `doses`: those deleted in the last 30 days, newest deletion first.
  - Today's prefill (D13) gains nothing: a dose's sheet has no fields to prefill.
- **P10: Installed apps keep working across the deploy.**
  - An app older than the Worker decodes `recent` one entry at a time and skips a kind it doesn't know (M1.8's P11).
  - Every other change is an added field.
  - `Household`, a stored response, doesn't change: `Me` carries `supplements` beside it, and `withSavedHousehold` keeps them across a household save.
  - The app persists no query cache, so `Me` always arrives with `supplements`.
- **P11: Today's Supplements card and the dose sheet (D3).**
  - The card sits under the feed card, with a row per supplement in use, in the household's order. Each row reads one of:
    - "Vitamin D not logged yet today", with its dose
    - "Vitamin D logged at 9:10 AM", then who and at what dose ("Saving…" until the household answers)
    - "Vitamin D logged twice today, last at …"
  - The card hides when no supplement is in use.
  - A row opens the dose sheet: the supplement's name as its title, "One tap saves it.", When (now, or Change), and "Log Vitamin D".
  - After a log ("Dose saved") or a delete ("Dose deleted"), a toast offers Undo for 5 seconds. Recently deleted's restore says "Dose restored", also with Undo.
  - A log that fails with no connection is held on the phone, through a reload, and reopens its sheet with the note, as a diaper's does. A second dose's 409 reopens the sheet on the question.
- **P12: A dose goes wherever a diaper goes on the phone.**
  - In Today's last 24 hours its row opens "Edit dose": When, Save, and Delete with Undo. The other phone's change shows "Save mine anyway".
  - Held entries (M5.6): `NEW_ENTRIES` and `TODAY_SHEETS`.
  - The optimistic layer: pending, taken back, saved, and swapped on Today and every cached day.
  - History: a Day marker with `PillIcon`, its details and Edit, and a Week lane of hollow dots after the diapers' (`left-[82%] w-[15%]`, with a legend entry and its day's name). A Supplements chip after Growth hides doses on every view.
  - History's chips wrap (`flex-wrap`). With four chips the row is 370 px wide, and 360 px phones would otherwise scroll sideways.
- **P13: Settings › Supplements (D1).**
  - The section sits after the child's and before the account. It lists the supplements in use ("Vitamin D · 400 IU"), then "Add a supplement", then a "Stopped" list.
  - The sheet has:
    - Name (up to 60 characters) and Dose (up to 40, may be empty)
    - the hint "Doses already logged keep the dose they were given at."
    - Save, or Add supplement
    - Stop giving it, or Give it again. Stopping asks first, inline as Delete does: "Stop giving Vitamin D? Today won't list it until you give it again.", with Stop, and Keep giving it, which takes focus. Giving it again saves at once.
  - It closes once saved.
  - Its fields are named `supplement-name` and `supplement-dose`, since the child's form already uses `name`.
- **P14: The CSV gains a row per live dose.**
  - Two columns sit after `Nap or night`: `Supplement` (its name now) and `Dose` (as given).
  - Kind is `Supplement`, and Start is the dose's time.
  - Every other row leaves those two cells empty.
- **P15: The bench.**
  - `seedTwoYears` adds one dose a day, nine hours after the time of day the bench runs at, and files it under the household's date then (`localDateOf` in its zone), as `logDose` does. The first version took the dose's UTC date, which is the next day whenever the bench runs between about 8 AM and 3 PM in Vancouver; the final fix wave corrected it.
  - `pnpm bench` times a dose write. With 730 doses it took 1 ms, and nothing else moved: Today 2 ms, this month 7 ms, a month a year ago 13 ms, the CSV 0.23 s.
- **P16: The List view (M3.9's hand-off).**
  - A dose is a row at its time among the feeds and diapers, in Today's row look, and opens its details with Edit. `recentRow` stays exported for the list, and takes the dose kind.
  - The Supplements chip hides the dose rows.
  - A day with only a measurement says "No feeds, diapers or supplements.".
- **P17: Sizes.**
  - The Worker grows from 260.73 to 262.82 KiB gzip, well under 400 KiB.
  - The app's script grows from 325.81 to 329.74 kB gzip. It's 330.96 kB at the head, with M3.9 and the second grill's changes, under the 350 kB limit.
  - Its CSS goes from 11.89 to 11.91 kB.
- **P18: History's Log at menu offers each supplement in use (the second grill).**
  - After Measurement, one button per supplement in use, with its name and `PillIcon`, opens that supplement's dose sheet. On the Day view it opens at the slot tapped. In the List view it opens at the same clock time on an earlier day, or at now on today (M3.9's rule).
  - A stopped supplement isn't offered.
  - `TimedSheet` gains `"dose"`, and `showAt(sheet, at, editing?)` carries the supplement, as `show` does.
  - The dose sheet needs no Today, so its buttons never wait.
  - M3.2 adds Sleep to the same menu and `"pastSleep"` to `TimedSheet`. Whichever merges second keeps both.

## Review Focus

These five are the most likely ways for M3.3 to fail a parent, each with the tests that pin it:

1. **Two phones and one day's dose.**
   - **Situation:** both parents give Vitamin D and tap Log within a second, or one logs while the other's phone is offline.
   - **Expect:** one dose saved unasked. The other phone asks "Vitamin D was logged at 9:10 AM by Emily. Record another dose anyway?", and "Don't record it" saves nothing.
   - **Pinned by:**
     - `doses.test.ts`: "lets only one of two phones' doses that day through unasked…" (Task 4)
     - `doses-core.test.ts`: "refuses a second dose of a supplement on the same date…" (Task 3)
     - `dose-session.test.ts`: "finds a second dose that day…" (Task 7)
     - `supplements.spec.ts`: "asks before a second dose that day…" and "logs nothing more when the parent doesn't mean a second dose" (Task 7)
2. **A dose near midnight, and a zone change.**
   - **Expect:** a dose at 11:50 PM and one at 12:10 AM fall on different days by the household's clock. Every dose is refiled under its date in a new zone.
   - **Pinned by:**
     - `doses-core.test.ts`: "files a dose either side of midnight…", "logs one dose of each supplement on a date, a dose just after midnight…" and "files every dose, deleted ones included, under its date in a new time zone…" (Task 3)
     - `periods-core.test.ts`: "lists the day's doses in time order, from its first moment to before the next day's…" (Task 5)
     - `dose-cache.test.ts`: "leaves today's doses alone for a dose backdated to yesterday…" and "moves a dose to its new date…" (Task 6)
3. **No connection at the tap.**
   - **Expect:** the dose is kept on the phone through a reload and asked about. It's saved under its own `actionId`, so a send that landed before the connection dropped isn't saved twice.
   - **Pinned by:**
     - `supplements.spec.ts`: "keeps a dose that failed with no connection through a reload…" (Task 7)
     - `doses.test.ts`: "answers a retry of a save whose answer was lost with the stored 201, and keeps one dose (AC 14)" (Task 4)
4. **The supplement changes under its doses.**
   - **Situation:** Andrew changes Vitamin D's dose or name, or stops it, while Emily's phone hasn't heard.
   - **Expect:**
     - past doses keep "400 IU"
     - History and the CSV name them by the supplement's name now
     - Today drops a stopped supplement
     - a save on top of the other phone's newer copy keeps what this parent didn't change
   - **Pinned by:**
     - `doses-core.test.ts`: "keeps each dose as its supplement's dose was then…" (Task 3)
     - `export-csv.test.ts`: "lists a dose with its supplement's name as it is now…" (Task 2)
     - `supplement-rows.test.ts`: "…leaving out one that's stopped" (Task 7)
     - `supplements-save.test.ts`: "puts its change on top of the other phone's newer copy once…" and "keeps the other phone's new name and stop…" (Task 6)
     - `settings-supplements.spec.ts`: "stops giving a supplement, which then leaves Today, and gives it again" and "keeps giving a supplement when the parent changes their mind about stopping it" (Task 9)
5. **The other phone sees it.**
   - **Expect:** a dose logged on one phone shows on the other's Today within 2 seconds (AC 6). An edit or delete from the other phone shows with "Save mine anyway".
   - **Pinned by:**
     - `supplements.spec.ts`: "shows a dose logged on one phone on the other within 2 seconds (AC 6)" (Task 7)
     - `edit-dose.spec.ts`: "says who changed the dose first, and saves mine on top of theirs when asked" (Task 8)

### Task 1: The supplement and the dose in the contract

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/supplements.ts`, `packages/contracts/test/supplements.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: contracts' `ActionId`, `Conflict`, `InvalidInput`, `NotFound`, `Name` (from `household.ts`), `Changed`, `Instant`, `LocalDate`, `Version` and `VersionedAction`.
- Produces, all exported from `@bt/contracts`:
  - `MAX_DOSE_LENGTH = 40`, and `DoseText`: a trimmed string of at most 40 characters, which may be empty
  - `Supplement`: `{ id, name: Name, dose: DoseText, stoppedAt: Instant | null, version, createdBy: string | null, createdAt, updatedBy: string | null, updatedAt }`
  - `NewSupplement` (`{ actionId, name, dose }`), `SupplementEdit` (`{ actionId, version, name, dose, stopped: boolean }`), `SavedSupplement` (`{ supplement, changed }`)
  - `SupplementConflict`, with `STALE_VERSION` or `ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH` and `current: Supplement`
  - `Dose`: `{ id, supplementId, occurredAt, localDate: LocalDate, dose: DoseText, version, createdBy, createdAt, updatedBy, updatedAt, deletedAt: Instant | null }`
  - `NewDose` (`{ actionId, supplementId, occurredAt, confirmedDuplicate: boolean }`), `DoseEdit` (`{ actionId, version, occurredAt }`), `SavedDose` (`{ dose, changed }`)
  - `DoseConflict`, with `SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY`, `STALE_VERSION` or `ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH` and `current: Dose`
  - `SupplementsGroup` (`addSupplement`, `editSupplement`) and `DosesGroup` (`logDose`, `editDose`, `deleteDose`, `restoreDose`). Neither joins `Api` yet: Tasks 2 and 4 add them with their handlers.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test.**

````ts
import { Exit, Result, Schema } from "effect"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import {
  Dose,
  DoseConflict,
  DoseEdit,
  DosesGroup,
  invalidInputFrom,
  MAX_DOSE_LENGTH,
  NewDose,
  NewSupplement,
  SavedDose,
  SavedSupplement,
  Supplement,
  SupplementConflict,
  SupplementEdit,
  SupplementsGroup,
} from "../src/index.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "8b2f9c3e-1d4a-4f6b-9e7c-5a3b2c1d0e9f"
const AT = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 8, 14, 30)

const VITAMIN_D: Supplement = {
  id: "supplement-1",
  name: "Vitamin D",
  dose: "400 IU",
  stoppedAt: null,
  version: 1,
  createdBy: null,
  createdAt: AT,
  updatedBy: null,
  updatedAt: AT,
}

const DOSE: Dose = {
  id: "dose-1",
  supplementId: "supplement-1",
  occurredAt: AT,
  localDate: "2026-10-08",
  dose: "400 IU",
  version: 1,
  createdBy: "caregiver-1",
  createdAt: AT,
  updatedBy: "caregiver-1",
  updatedAt: AT,
  deletedAt: null,
}

const fieldErrors = <A, I extends Schema.Json>(schema: Schema.Codec<A, I>, input: Schema.Json) => {
  const decoded = Schema.decodeUnknownResult(Schema.toCodecJson(schema), { errors: "all" })(input)
  return Result.isFailure(decoded) ? invalidInputFrom(decoded.failure.issue).fieldErrors : []
}

const endpointsOf = (group: { readonly endpoints: Record<string, { method: string; path: string }> }) =>
  Object.entries(group.endpoints).map(([name, { method, path }]) => `${name} ${method} ${path}`)

describe("a supplement", () => {
  it("is a name and a dose, given until it's stopped, with no author when the household seeded it", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(Supplement, VITAMIN_D)).toEqual([])
    expect(fieldErrors(Supplement, { ...VITAMIN_D, stoppedAt: AT, createdBy: "caregiver-1" })).toEqual([])
  })

  it("may have no dose written down", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(Supplement, { ...VITAMIN_D, dose: "" })).toEqual([])
  })

  it("is answered with the keys its save changed", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(SavedSupplement, { supplement: VITAMIN_D, changed: [["me"]] })).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe("a new supplement", () => {
  it("carries a name and a dose", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(NewSupplement, { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" })).toEqual([])
  })

  it(`refuses an empty or padded name, and a dose longer than ${MAX_DOSE_LENGTH} characters`, () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(NewSupplement, { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: "", dose: "x".repeat(41) })).toEqual([
      { path: "name", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "dose", issue: "invalid" },
    ])
    expect(fieldErrors(NewSupplement, { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: " Iron", dose: "1 mL " })).toEqual([
      { path: "name", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "dose", issue: "invalid" },
    ])
  })
})

describe("an edit to a supplement", () => {
  it("carries its name, its dose, whether it's stopped, and the version it was made from", () => {
    const edit = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "800 IU", stopped: true }
    expect(fieldErrors(SupplementEdit, edit)).toEqual([])
    expect(fieldErrors(SupplementEdit, { ...edit, version: 0, stopped: "yes" })).toEqual([
      { path: "version", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "stopped", issue: "invalid" },
    ])
  })
})

describe("SupplementConflict", () => {
  const decode = Schema.decodeUnknownExit(Schema.toCodecJson(SupplementConflict))

  it.each(["STALE_VERSION", "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH"])("carries %s with the supplement it's about", (code) => {
    const exit = decode({ _tag: "Conflict", code, current: VITAMIN_D })
    expect(Exit.isSuccess(exit) && exit.value.current).toEqual(VITAMIN_D)
  })

  it("refuses a code a supplement can't conflict on", () => {
    expect(Exit.isFailure(decode({ _tag: "Conflict", code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current: VITAMIN_D }))).toBe(
      true,
    )
  })
})

describe("a dose", () => {
  it("is a supplement given at a time, on the household's date then, with the dose as it was written", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(Dose, DOSE)).toEqual([])
    expect(fieldErrors(Dose, { ...DOSE, localDate: "2026-10-32" })).toEqual([{ path: "localDate", issue: "invalid" }])
  })

  it("is answered with the keys its save changed", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(SavedDose, { dose: DOSE, changed: [["today"], ["day", "2026-10-08"]] })).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe("a new dose", () => {
  it("names its supplement and its time, and whether a second dose that day was meant", () => {
    const dose = { actionId: ACTION_ID, supplementId: "supplement-1", occurredAt: AT, confirmedDuplicate: false }
    expect(fieldErrors(NewDose, dose)).toEqual([])
    expect(fieldErrors(NewDose, { ...dose, confirmedDuplicate: true })).toEqual([])
  })

  it("names every field that's missing or bad", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(NewDose, { actionId: "first", occurredAt: AT / 1000 })).toEqual([
      { path: "actionId", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "supplementId", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "occurredAt", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "confirmedDuplicate", issue: "invalid" },
    ])
  })
})

describe("an edit to a dose", () => {
  it("moves its time, from the version it was made from", () => {
    expect(fieldErrors(DoseEdit, { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, occurredAt: AT })).toEqual([])
    expect(fieldErrors(DoseEdit, { actionId: ACTION_ID, occurredAt: AT })).toEqual([
      { path: "version", issue: "invalid" },
    ])
  })
})

describe("DoseConflict", () => {
  const decode = Schema.decodeUnknownExit(Schema.toCodecJson(DoseConflict))

  it.each(["SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", "STALE_VERSION", "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH"])(
    "carries %s with the dose it's about",
    (code) => {
      const exit = decode({ _tag: "Conflict", code, current: DOSE })
      expect(Exit.isSuccess(exit) && exit.value.current).toEqual(DOSE)
    },
  )

  it("refuses a code a dose can't conflict on", () => {
    expect(Exit.isFailure(decode({ _tag: "Conflict", code: "FEED_EMPTY", current: DOSE }))).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe("the supplement endpoints", () => {
  it("add a supplement and edit one, stopping it included", () => {
    expect(endpointsOf(SupplementsGroup)).toEqual([
      "addSupplement POST /supplements",
      "editSupplement PATCH /supplements/:id",
    ])
  })

  it("log, edit, delete and restore a dose", () => {
    expect(endpointsOf(DosesGroup)).toEqual([
      "logDose POST /doses",
      "editDose PATCH /doses/:id",
      "deleteDose DELETE /doses/:id",
      "restoreDose POST /doses/:id/restore",
    ])
  })
})
````

- [ ] **Step 3: The contract.**

````ts
import { Schema } from "effect"
import { HttpApiEndpoint, HttpApiGroup, HttpApiSchema } from "effect/http-api"
import { ActionId } from "./action-id.ts"
import { Conflict, InvalidInput, NotFound } from "./errors.ts"
import { Name } from "./household.ts"
import { Changed } from "./query-keys.ts"
import { Instant, LocalDate } from "./time.ts"
import { Version, VersionedAction } from "./version.ts"

export const MAX_DOSE_LENGTH = 40

export const DoseText = Schema.String.check(Schema.isTrimmed(), Schema.isMaxLength(MAX_DOSE_LENGTH))
export type DoseText = typeof DoseText.Type

export const Supplement = Schema.Struct({
  id: Schema.String,
  name: Name,
  dose: DoseText,
  stoppedAt: Schema.NullOr(Instant),
  version: Version,
  createdBy: Schema.NullOr(Schema.String),
  createdAt: Instant,
  updatedBy: Schema.NullOr(Schema.String),
  updatedAt: Instant,
})
export type Supplement = typeof Supplement.Type

export const NewSupplement = Schema.Struct({ actionId: ActionId, name: Name, dose: DoseText })
export type NewSupplement = typeof NewSupplement.Type

export const SupplementEdit = Schema.Struct({
  actionId: ActionId,
  version: Version,
  name: Name,
  dose: DoseText,
  stopped: Schema.Boolean,
})
export type SupplementEdit = typeof SupplementEdit.Type

export const SavedSupplement = Schema.Struct({ supplement: Supplement, changed: Changed })
export type SavedSupplement = typeof SavedSupplement.Type

export class SupplementConflict extends Conflict<SupplementConflict>()(
  "SupplementConflict",
  ["STALE_VERSION", "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH"],
  Supplement,
) {}

export const Dose = Schema.Struct({
  id: Schema.String,
  supplementId: Schema.String,
  occurredAt: Instant,
  localDate: LocalDate,
  dose: DoseText,
  version: Version,
  createdBy: Schema.String,
  createdAt: Instant,
  updatedBy: Schema.String,
  updatedAt: Instant,
  deletedAt: Schema.NullOr(Instant),
})
export type Dose = typeof Dose.Type

export const NewDose = Schema.Struct({
  actionId: ActionId,
  supplementId: Schema.String,
  occurredAt: Instant,
  confirmedDuplicate: Schema.Boolean,
})
export type NewDose = typeof NewDose.Type

export const DoseEdit = Schema.Struct({ actionId: ActionId, version: Version, occurredAt: Instant })
export type DoseEdit = typeof DoseEdit.Type

export const SavedDose = Schema.Struct({ dose: Dose, changed: Changed })
export type SavedDose = typeof SavedDose.Type

export class DoseConflict extends Conflict<DoseConflict>()(
  "DoseConflict",
  ["SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", "STALE_VERSION", "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH"],
  Dose,
) {}

const byId = { id: Schema.String }

export class SupplementsGroup extends HttpApiGroup.make("supplements")
  .add(
    HttpApiEndpoint.post("addSupplement", "/supplements", {
      payload: NewSupplement,
      success: SavedSupplement.pipe(HttpApiSchema.status(201)),
      error: [InvalidInput, SupplementConflict],
    }),
  )
  .add(
    HttpApiEndpoint.patch("editSupplement", "/supplements/:id", {
      params: byId,
      payload: SupplementEdit,
      success: SavedSupplement,
      error: [InvalidInput, NotFound, SupplementConflict],
    }),
  ) {}

const onDose = { params: byId, success: SavedDose, error: [InvalidInput, NotFound, DoseConflict] }

export class DosesGroup extends HttpApiGroup.make("doses")
  .add(
    HttpApiEndpoint.post("logDose", "/doses", {
      payload: NewDose,
      success: SavedDose.pipe(HttpApiSchema.status(201)),
      error: [InvalidInput, NotFound, DoseConflict],
    }),
  )
  .add(HttpApiEndpoint.patch("editDose", "/doses/:id", { ...onDose, payload: DoseEdit }))
  .add(HttpApiEndpoint.delete("deleteDose", "/doses/:id", { ...onDose, payload: VersionedAction }))
  .add(HttpApiEndpoint.post("restoreDose", "/doses/:id/restore", { ...onDose, payload: VersionedAction })) {}
````

````diff
+  NewDose,
+  NewSupplement,
+  SavedDose,
+  SavedSupplement,
+  Supplement,
+  SupplementConflict,
+  SupplementEdit,
+  SupplementsGroup,
+} from "./supplements.ts"
 export { Instant, IsoWeek, LocalDate, TimeZone, YearMonth } from "./time.ts"
 export { Last24h, Prefill, RecentEntries, RecentEntry, Today, TodayGroup, Totals } from "./today.ts"
 export { Version, VersionedAction } from "./version.ts"
````

### Task 2: The supplements tables, the list in `/api/me`, editing a supplement, and doses on the CSV

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/worker/src/db/migrations/0008-supplements.sql`, `apps/worker/src/core/supplements/definitions.ts`, `apps/worker/src/core/supplements/doses.ts`, `apps/worker/src/supplement-handlers.ts`, `apps/worker/test/supplement-definitions.test.ts`, `apps/worker/test/supplements-table.test.ts`, `apps/worker/test/supplements.test.ts`
- Modify:
  - `apps/worker/src/db/migrations.ts`, `apps/worker/src/core/household.ts`, `apps/worker/src/core/export-csv.ts`, `apps/worker/src/http.ts`
  - `packages/contracts/src/me.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/api.ts`
  - `apps/web/src/api/settings.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/hooks.ts`
  - `docs/api.json` (generated)
- Test:
  - `apps/worker/test/export-csv.test.ts`, `export.test.ts`, `me.test.ts`, `migrate.test.ts`
  - `packages/contracts/test/api.test.ts`
  - `apps/web/test/settings.test.ts`, `client.test.ts`, `query-client.test.ts`, `growth-fixtures.ts`
  - `e2e/shell.spec.ts`, `e2e/settings.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 1's schemas
  - M3.1's CSV columns, which end `Asleep (min)`, `Nap or night`
  - `mutations`, `Stamp`, `CoreResult` and `queryKeys.me`
- Produces:
  - `core/supplements/definitions.ts`:
    - `supplementsOf(sql): Supplement[]`, in the order they were added
    - `seedVitaminD(sql, now)`
    - `addSupplement(stamp, entry: NewSupplement)(sql): CoreResult<Supplement, never>`
    - `editSupplement(stamp, id, edit: SupplementEdit)(sql): CoreResult<Supplement, NotFound | SupplementConflict>`
  - `core/supplements/doses.ts`: `DoseRow`, `DOSE_COLUMNS`, `doseOf(row): Dose` and `liveDosesSince(sql, from): Dose[]` (oldest first). Tasks 3 and 5 add to this file.
  - `supplementHandlers(storage, broadcast)`, and `SupplementsGroup` in `Api`
  - `Me`'s `household.supplements: Supplement[]`
  - the web's `withSavedHousehold(me: Me, household: Household): Me`, which keeps `Me`'s caregivers and supplements across a household save

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing Worker tests.** The tables and the seed:

````ts
import { runInDurableObject } from "cloudflare:test"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { migrate } from "../src/db/migrate.ts"
import { MIGRATIONS } from "../src/db/migrations.ts"
import { ANDREW, caregiverId, inHousehold, NOW, seededHousehold } from "./fixtures.ts"

const SUPPLEMENTS_MIGRATION = MIGRATIONS.findIndex(({ name }) => name === "0008-supplements") + 1

const supplementNames = (sql: SqlStorage) =>
  sql
    .exec<{ name: string; dose: string; created_by: string | null }>(
      "SELECT name, dose, created_by FROM supplements ORDER BY rowid",
    )
    .toArray()

const insertSupplement = (sql: SqlStorage, name: string, dose: string) =>
  sql.exec(
    "INSERT INTO supplements (id, name, dose, version, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?)",
    crypto.randomUUID(),
    name,
    dose,
    NOW,
    NOW,
  )

const insertDose = (sql: SqlStorage, supplementId: string, localDate: string) => {
  const andrew = caregiverId(sql, ANDREW.email)
  sql.exec(
    `INSERT INTO supplement_logs
       (id, supplement_id, occurred_at, local_date, dose, version, created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, '400 IU', 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
    crypto.randomUUID(),
    supplementId,
    NOW,
    localDate,
    andrew,
    NOW,
    andrew,
    NOW,
  )
}

describe("0008-supplements", () => {
  it("gives a household that came before it Vitamin D at 400 IU once, with no author", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const after = await runInDurableObject(household, (_instance, state) => {
      const { storage } = state
      storage.sql.exec("DROP TABLE supplement_logs")
      storage.sql.exec("DROP TABLE supplements")
      storage.sql.exec("DELETE FROM _migrations WHERE id = ?", SUPPLEMENTS_MIGRATION)
      migrate(storage, MIGRATIONS, NOW)
      migrate(storage, MIGRATIONS, NOW)
      return supplementNames(storage.sql)
    })
    expect(after).toEqual([{ name: "Vitamin D", dose: "400 IU", created_by: null }])
  })
})

describe("the supplements tables", () => {
  it("refuse a supplement with no name or a dose longer than 40 characters", async () => {
    await inHousehold((sql) => {
      expect(() => insertSupplement(sql, "", "1 mL")).toThrow("CHECK constraint failed")
      expect(() => insertSupplement(sql, "Iron", "x".repeat(41))).toThrow("CHECK constraint failed")
    })
  })

  it("refuse a dose of a supplement the household doesn't hold, or with a date that isn't one", async () => {
    await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const [vitaminD] = sql.exec<{ id: string }>("SELECT id FROM supplements").toArray()
      if (vitaminD === undefined) throw new Error("expected the seeded Vitamin D")
      expect(() => insertDose(sql, "no-such-supplement", "2026-10-08")).toThrow("FOREIGN KEY constraint failed")
      expect(() => insertDose(sql, vitaminD.id, "8 Oct 2026")).toThrow("CHECK constraint failed")
      insertDose(sql, vitaminD.id, "2026-10-08")
    })
  })
})
````

The definitions, added and edited in the core:

````ts
import { NotFound, SupplementConflict } from "@bt/contracts"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { addSupplement, editSupplement, supplementsOf } from "../src/core/supplements/definitions.ts"
import { ACTION_ID, ANDREW, caregiverId, EMILY, inHousehold, NOW, saved, stampOf } from "./fixtures.ts"

const LATER = NOW + 60_000

const iron = { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" }

describe("a new household's supplements", () => {
  it("hold Vitamin D at 400 IU, seeded with no author and given until it's stopped", async () => {
    expect(await inHousehold((sql) => supplementsOf(sql))).toEqual([
      {
        id: expect.any(String),
        name: "Vitamin D",
        dose: "400 IU",
        stoppedAt: null,
        version: 1,
        createdBy: null,
        createdAt: expect.any(Number),
        updatedBy: null,
        updatedAt: expect.any(Number),
      },
    ])
  })
})

describe("addSupplement", () => {
  it("adds a supplement as version 1, stamped with who added it, after the ones already there", async () => {
    const { result, listed, andrew } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const result = addSupplement(stampOf(sql), iron)(sql)
      return { result, listed: supplementsOf(sql).map(({ name }) => name), andrew: caregiverId(sql, ANDREW.email) }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: {
        id: expect.any(String),
        name: "Iron",
        dose: "1 mL",
        stoppedAt: null,
        version: 1,
        createdBy: andrew,
        createdAt: NOW,
        updatedBy: andrew,
        updatedAt: NOW,
      },
      changed: [["me"]],
    })
    expect(listed).toEqual(["Vitamin D", "Iron"])
  })
})

describe("editSupplement", () => {
  it("renames a supplement and changes its dose as the next version, stamped with who saved it", async () => {
    const { added, edited, emily } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const added = saved(addSupplement(stampOf(sql), iron)(sql))
      const edit = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, name: "Iron drops", dose: "0.5 mL", stopped: false }
      const edited = editSupplement(stampOf(sql, EMILY.email, LATER), added.id, edit)(sql)
      return { added, edited, emily: caregiverId(sql, EMILY.email) }
    })
    expect(edited).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: { ...added, name: "Iron drops", dose: "0.5 mL", version: 2, updatedBy: emily, updatedAt: LATER },
      changed: [["me"]],
    })
  })

  it("stops a supplement when it's saved stopped, keeps the first stop's time, and gives it again when saved given", async () => {
    const { stopped, still, given } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const added = saved(addSupplement(stampOf(sql), iron)(sql))
      const edit = (version: number, stopped: boolean, now: number) =>
        saved(editSupplement(stampOf(sql, ANDREW.email, now), added.id, { ...iron, version, stopped })(sql))
      return { stopped: edit(1, true, NOW), still: edit(2, true, LATER), given: edit(3, false, LATER) }
    })
    expect(stopped.stoppedAt).toBe(NOW)
    expect(still.stoppedAt).toBe(NOW)
    expect(given.stoppedAt).toBeNull()
  })

  it("answers STALE_VERSION with the supplement as it is now, and changes nothing", async () => {
    const { result, current } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const [vitaminD] = supplementsOf(sql)
      if (vitaminD === undefined) throw new Error("expected the seeded Vitamin D")
      const stale = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "800 IU", stopped: false }
      return { result: editSupplement(stampOf(sql), vitaminD.id, stale)(sql), current: vitaminD }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: new SupplementConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current }) })
  })

  it("answers NotFound for a supplement the household doesn't hold", async () => {
    const result = await inHousehold((sql) =>
      editSupplement(stampOf(sql), "no-such-supplement", { ...iron, version: 1, stopped: false })(sql),
    )
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: new NotFound() })
  })
})
````

The two endpoints:

````ts
import { Me, SavedSupplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import { Schema } from "effect"
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest"
import { ANDREW, EMILY, NOW, seededHousehold } from "./fixtures.ts"
import { type Household, get, inStorage, send, sendJson } from "./requests.ts"

const decodeSaved = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(Schema.toCodecJson(SavedSupplement))
const decodeMe = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(Schema.toCodecJson(Me))

const iron = () => ({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" })

const caregiverId = (household: Household, email: string) =>
  inStorage(household, (sql) => sql.exec<{ id: string }>("SELECT id FROM caregivers WHERE email = ?", email).one().id)

const added = async (household: Household) =>
  decodeSaved((await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/supplements", iron())).body).supplement

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] })
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW)
})

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
})

describe("POST /api/supplements", () => {
  it("adds a supplement and answers 201, no-store, with it and the key it changed", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const response = await send(household, "POST", "/api/supplements", JSON.stringify(iron()))
    const andrew = await caregiverId(household, ANDREW.email)
    expect(response.status).toBe(201)
    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store")
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({
      supplement: {
        id: expect.any(String),
        name: "Iron",
        dose: "1 mL",
        stoppedAt: null,
        version: 1,
        createdBy: andrew,
        createdAt: NOW,
        updatedBy: andrew,
        updatedAt: NOW,
      },
      changed: [["me"]],
    })
  })

  it("answers a retry with the same answer, and adds the supplement once", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const request = iron()
    const first = await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/supplements", request)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/supplements", request)).toEqual(first)
    const me = decodeMe(await (await get(household, "/api/me", ANDREW.email)).json())
    expect(me.household.supplements.map(({ name }) => name)).toEqual(["Vitamin D", "Iron"])
  })

  it("answers 400 naming name for a name that's empty", async () => {
    expect(await sendJson(await seededHousehold(), "POST", "/api/supplements", { ...iron(), name: "" })).toEqual({
      status: 400,
      body: { _tag: "InvalidInput", fieldErrors: [{ path: "name", issue: "invalid" }] },
    })
  })
})

describe("PATCH /api/supplements/:id", () => {
  it("renames, re-doses and stops a supplement, answering it as the next version", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const supplement = await added(household)
    const edit = { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 1, name: "Iron drops", dose: "0.5 mL", stopped: true }
    const emily = await caregiverId(household, EMILY.email)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "PATCH", `/api/supplements/${supplement.id}`, edit, EMILY.email)).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: {
        supplement: {
          ...supplement,
          name: "Iron drops",
          dose: "0.5 mL",
          stoppedAt: NOW,
          version: 2,
          updatedBy: emily,
          updatedAt: NOW,
        },
        changed: [["me"]],
      },
    })
  })

  it("answers 409 STALE_VERSION with the supplement as it is now", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const supplement = await added(household)
    const stale = { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 3, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL", stopped: false }
    expect(await sendJson(household, "PATCH", `/api/supplements/${supplement.id}`, stale)).toEqual({
      status: 409,
      body: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: supplement },
    })
  })

  it("answers 404 for a supplement the household doesn't hold", async () => {
    const edit = { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 1, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL", stopped: false }
    expect(await sendJson(await seededHousehold(), "PATCH", "/api/supplements/no-such-supplement", edit)).toEqual({
      status: 404,
      body: { _tag: "NotFound" },
    })
  })
})
````

````sql
CREATE TABLE supplements (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(name) BETWEEN 1 AND 60),
  dose TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(dose) <= 40),
  stopped_at INTEGER CHECK (stopped_at >= 946684800000 AND stopped_at < 4102444800000),
  version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (version >= 1),
  created_by TEXT REFERENCES caregivers (id),
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (created_at >= 946684800000 AND created_at < 4102444800000),
  updated_by TEXT REFERENCES caregivers (id),
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (updated_at >= 946684800000 AND updated_at < 4102444800000)
) STRICT;

INSERT INTO supplements (id, name, dose, version, created_at, updated_at)
SELECT 'b1a6d1e4-0c7e-4d3a-9f2b-5e8c7a6d4f31', 'Vitamin D', '400 IU', 1,
  CAST(unixepoch('subsec') * 1000 AS INTEGER), CAST(unixepoch('subsec') * 1000 AS INTEGER)
FROM household;

CREATE TABLE supplement_logs (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  supplement_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES supplements (id),
  occurred_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (occurred_at >= 946684800000 AND occurred_at < 4102444800000),
  local_date TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (local_date GLOB '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]'),
  dose TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(dose) <= 40),
  version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (version >= 1),
  created_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES caregivers (id),
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (created_at >= 946684800000 AND created_at < 4102444800000),
  updated_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES caregivers (id),
  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (updated_at >= 946684800000 AND updated_at < 4102444800000),
  deleted_at INTEGER CHECK (deleted_at >= 946684800000 AND deleted_at < 4102444800000)
) STRICT;

CREATE INDEX supplement_logs_by_date ON supplement_logs (local_date, supplement_id);

CREATE INDEX supplement_logs_by_time ON supplement_logs (occurred_at);
````

````ts
import {
  type Changed,
  type NewSupplement,
  NotFound,
  queryKeys,
  type Supplement,
  SupplementConflict,
  type SupplementEdit,
} from "@bt/contracts"
import type { CoreResult, Stamp } from "../receipts.ts"

type SupplementRow = {
  id: string
  name: string
  dose: string
  stopped_at: number | null
  version: number
  created_by: string | null
  created_at: number
  updated_by: string | null
  updated_at: number
}

const COLUMNS = "id, name, dose, stopped_at, version, created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at"

const CHANGED: Changed = [queryKeys.me]

const supplementOf = (row: SupplementRow): Supplement => ({
  id: row.id,
  name: row.name,
  dose: row.dose,
  stoppedAt: row.stopped_at,
  version: row.version,
  createdBy: row.created_by,
  createdAt: row.created_at,
  updatedBy: row.updated_by,
  updatedAt: row.updated_at,
})

export const supplementsOf = (sql: SqlStorage): Supplement[] =>
  sql.exec<SupplementRow>(`SELECT ${COLUMNS} FROM supplements ORDER BY rowid`).toArray().map(supplementOf)

export const seedVitaminD = (sql: SqlStorage, now: number) =>
  sql.exec(
    "INSERT INTO supplements (id, name, dose, version, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, 'Vitamin D', '400 IU', 1, ?, ?)",
    crypto.randomUUID(),
    now,
    now,
  )

export const addSupplement =
  (stamp: Stamp, entry: NewSupplement) =>
  (sql: SqlStorage): CoreResult<Supplement, never> => {
    const row = sql
      .exec<SupplementRow>(
        `INSERT INTO supplements (id, name, dose, version, created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING ${COLUMNS}`,
        crypto.randomUUID(),
        entry.name,
        entry.dose,
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
      )
      .one()
    return { ok: true, response: supplementOf(row), changed: CHANGED }
  }

const stoppedAt = (row: SupplementRow, stopped: boolean, now: number) => (stopped ? (row.stopped_at ?? now) : null)

export const editSupplement =
  (stamp: Stamp, id: string, edit: SupplementEdit) =>
  (sql: SqlStorage): CoreResult<Supplement, NotFound | SupplementConflict> => {
    const [current] = sql.exec<SupplementRow>(`SELECT ${COLUMNS} FROM supplements WHERE id = ?`, id).toArray()
    if (current === undefined) return { ok: false, error: new NotFound() }
    if (current.version !== edit.version) {
      return { ok: false, error: new SupplementConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current: supplementOf(current) }) }
    }
    const row = sql
      .exec<SupplementRow>(
        `UPDATE supplements SET name = ?, dose = ?, stopped_at = ?, version = version + 1, updated_by = ?, updated_at = ?
         WHERE id = ? RETURNING ${COLUMNS}`,
        edit.name,
        edit.dose,
        stoppedAt(current, edit.stopped, stamp.now),
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
        id,
      )
      .one()
    return { ok: true, response: supplementOf(row), changed: CHANGED }
  }
````

````ts
import type { Dose } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { Instant } from "@bt/domain"

export type DoseRow = {
  id: string
  supplement_id: string
  occurred_at: number
  local_date: string
  dose: string
  version: number
  created_by: string
  created_at: number
  updated_by: string
  updated_at: number
  deleted_at: number | null
}

export const DOSE_COLUMNS =
  "id, supplement_id, occurred_at, local_date, dose, version, created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at, deleted_at"

export const doseOf = (row: DoseRow): Dose => ({
  id: row.id,
  supplementId: row.supplement_id,
  occurredAt: row.occurred_at,
  localDate: row.local_date,
  dose: row.dose,
  version: row.version,
  createdBy: row.created_by,
  createdAt: row.created_at,
  updatedBy: row.updated_by,
  updatedAt: row.updated_at,
  deletedAt: row.deleted_at,
})

export const liveDosesSince = (sql: SqlStorage, from: Instant): Dose[] =>
  sql
    .exec<DoseRow>(
      `SELECT ${DOSE_COLUMNS} FROM supplement_logs WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND occurred_at >= ?
       ORDER BY occurred_at, rowid`,
      from,
    )
    .toArray()
    .map(doseOf)
````

````ts
import { Api, NewSupplement, Supplement, SupplementConflict, SupplementEdit } from "@bt/contracts"
import { Effect } from "effect"
import { HttpApiBuilder } from "effect/http-api"
import { addSupplement, editSupplement } from "./core/supplements/definitions.ts"
import { mutations, type Saved } from "./mutations.ts"
import type { Broadcast } from "./sockets.ts"

const asSavedSupplement = Effect.map(({ record, changed }: Saved<Supplement>) => ({ supplement: record, changed }))

export const supplementHandlers = (storage: DurableObjectStorage, broadcast: Broadcast) => {
  const mutate = mutations(
    storage,
    broadcast,
    Supplement,
    (saved) => new SupplementConflict({ code: "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH", current: saved }),
  )
  return HttpApiBuilder.group(Api, "supplements", (handlers) =>
    handlers
      .handle("addSupplement", ({ payload, request }) =>
        mutate(request, NewSupplement, payload, (stamp) => addSupplement(stamp, payload)).pipe(asSavedSupplement),
      )
      .handle("editSupplement", ({ params, payload, request }) =>
        mutate(request, SupplementEdit, payload, (stamp) => editSupplement(stamp, params.id, payload)).pipe(
          asSavedSupplement,
        ),
      ),
  )
}
````

### Task 3: Logging, moving, deleting and restoring a dose

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/worker/src/core/supplements/dose-writes.ts`, `apps/worker/test/doses-core.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/worker/src/core/household-edits.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2's `DoseRow`, `DOSE_COLUMNS`, `doseOf` and `supplementsOf`
  - the core's `calendarOf`, `changedKeys` and `timeIssue` (`core/calendar.ts`), and the domain's `localDateOf`
- Produces, in `core/supplements/dose-writes.ts`:
  - `logDose(stamp, entry: NewDose)(sql)`
  - `editDose(stamp, id, edit: DoseEdit)(sql)`
  - `deleteDose(stamp, id, action: VersionedAction)(sql)` and `restoreDose(stamp, id, action: VersionedAction)(sql)`
  - Each returns `CoreResult<Dose, NotFound | InvalidInput | DoseConflict>`.
  - `refileDoses(sql, zone)`, which `editHousehold` calls when the zone changes (P7)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test.**

````ts
import { DoseConflict, InvalidInput, type NewDose, NotFound } from "@bt/contracts"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { editChild, editHousehold } from "../src/core/household-edits.ts"
import { addSupplement, editSupplement, supplementsOf } from "../src/core/supplements/definitions.ts"
import { deleteDose, editDose, logDose, restoreDose } from "../src/core/supplements/dose-writes.ts"
import {
  ACTION_ID,
  ANDREW,
  CHANGED_TODAY,
  caregiverId,
  EMILY,
  inHousehold,
  MINUTE_MS,
  minutesAgo,
  movedBirthDate,
  NOW,
  OCT_1_AT_00_30,
  SEED,
  SEP_3_BEGINS,
  SEP_30_AT_23_30,
  saved,
  stampOf,
} from "./fixtures.ts"

const vitaminDOf = (sql: SqlStorage) => {
  const [vitaminD] = supplementsOf(sql)
  if (vitaminD === undefined) throw new Error("expected the seeded Vitamin D")
  return vitaminD
}

const doseOf = (supplementId: string, occurredAt = minutesAgo(60), confirmedDuplicate = false): NewDose => ({
  actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
  supplementId,
  occurredAt,
  confirmedDuplicate,
})

const dosed = (sql: SqlStorage, occurredAt = minutesAgo(60), confirmedDuplicate = false, email = ANDREW.email) =>
  saved(logDose(stampOf(sql, email), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id, occurredAt, confirmedDuplicate))(sql))

const versioned = (version: number) => ({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version })

const doseRows = (sql: SqlStorage) => sql.exec("SELECT id FROM supplement_logs").toArray().length

describe("logging a dose", () => {
  it("saves it as version 1 under the household's date then, with its supplement's dose, stamped with who logged it", async () => {
    const { result, vitaminD, andrew } = await inHousehold((sql) => ({
      result: logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id))(sql),
      vitaminD: vitaminDOf(sql),
      andrew: caregiverId(sql, ANDREW.email),
    }))
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: {
        id: expect.any(String),
        supplementId: vitaminD.id,
        occurredAt: minutesAgo(60),
        localDate: "2026-10-08",
        dose: "400 IU",
        version: 1,
        createdBy: andrew,
        createdAt: NOW,
        updatedBy: andrew,
        updatedAt: NOW,
        deletedAt: null,
      },
      changed: CHANGED_TODAY,
    })
  })

  it("files a dose either side of midnight under the household's date, not UTC's", async () => {
    const dates = await inHousehold((sql) => [
      dosed(sql, SEP_30_AT_23_30).localDate,
      dosed(sql, OCT_1_AT_00_30).localDate,
    ])
    expect(dates).toEqual(["2026-09-30", "2026-10-01"])
  })

  it("refuses a second dose of a supplement on the same date with SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY and the dose already logged, saving nothing", async () => {
    const { first, second, rows } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const first = dosed(sql, minutesAgo(180), false, EMILY.email)
      const second = logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id, minutesAgo(30)))(sql)
      return { first, second, rows: doseRows(sql) }
    })
    expect(second).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new DoseConflict({ code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current: first }),
    })
    expect(rows).toBe(1)
  })

  it("logs the second dose when the parent confirms it, and answers the later of two with a third", async () => {
    const { second, third } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      dosed(sql, minutesAgo(180))
      const second = dosed(sql, minutesAgo(60), true)
      return { second, third: logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id, minutesAgo(10)))(sql) }
    })
    expect(third).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new DoseConflict({ code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current: second }),
    })
  })

  it("logs one dose of each supplement on a date, a dose just after midnight, and one after a deleted dose, without asking", async () => {
    const results = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const iron = saved(addSupplement(stampOf(sql), { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" })(sql))
      const deleted = dosed(sql, minutesAgo(300))
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), deleted.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      dosed(sql, SEP_30_AT_23_30)
      return [
        logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id, minutesAgo(60)))(sql).ok,
        logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(iron.id, minutesAgo(30)))(sql).ok,
        logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminDOf(sql).id, OCT_1_AT_00_30))(sql).ok,
      ]
    })
    expect(results).toEqual([true, true, true])
  })

  it("keeps each dose as its supplement's dose was then, when that dose changes later", async () => {
    const doses = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const before = dosed(sql, SEP_30_AT_23_30)
      const vitaminD = vitaminDOf(sql)
      const edit = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, name: vitaminD.name, dose: "800 IU", stopped: false }
      saved(editSupplement(stampOf(sql), vitaminD.id, edit)(sql))
      return [before.dose, dosed(sql).dose]
    })
    expect(doses).toEqual(["400 IU", "800 IU"])
  })

  it("answers NotFound for a supplement the household doesn't hold", async () => {
    const result = await inHousehold((sql) => logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf("no-such-supplement"))(sql))
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: new NotFound() })
  })

  it("refuses a time more than 5 minutes ahead of the server's clock, and one before the birth date", async () => {
    const results = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const vitaminD = vitaminDOf(sql).id
      return [
        logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminD, NOW + 6 * MINUTE_MS))(sql),
        logDose(stampOf(sql), doseOf(vitaminD, Date.UTC(2026, 7, 31, 12)))(sql),
      ]
    })
    expect(results).toEqual([
      { ok: false, error: new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "occurredAt", issue: "in_future" }] }) },
      { ok: false, error: new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "occurredAt", issue: "before_birth" }] }) },
    ])
  })
})

describe("editing a dose", () => {
  it("moves its time and its date, as the next version stamped with who edited it, and changes both days", async () => {
    const { logged, result, emily } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql, OCT_1_AT_00_30)
      const edit = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: SEP_30_AT_23_30 }
      return {
        logged,
        result: editDose(stampOf(sql, EMILY.email, NOW + MINUTE_MS), logged.id, edit)(sql),
        emily: caregiverId(sql, EMILY.email),
      }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: {
        ...logged,
        occurredAt: SEP_30_AT_23_30,
        localDate: "2026-09-30",
        version: 2,
        updatedBy: emily,
        updatedAt: NOW + MINUTE_MS,
      },
      changed: [
        ["today"],
        ["last24h"],
        ["day", "2026-09-30"],
        ["day", "2026-10-01"],
        ["week", "2026-W40"],
        ["month", "2026-09"],
        ["month", "2026-10"],
      ],
    })
  })

  it("moves a dose onto a date that already has one without asking, since moving a dose gives no dose", async () => {
    const result = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      dosed(sql, minutesAgo(600))
      const moved = dosed(sql, SEP_30_AT_23_30)
      return editDose(stampOf(sql), moved.id, { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: minutesAgo(30) })(sql)
    })
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it("refuses to move it before the birth date, and answers STALE_VERSION from an older version", async () => {
    const results = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql)
      const early = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: Date.UTC(2026, 7, 31, 12) }
      return {
        early: editDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, early)(sql),
        stale: editDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, { ...early, version: 2, occurredAt: minutesAgo(5) })(sql),
        logged,
      }
    })
    expect(results.early).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "occurredAt", issue: "before_birth" }] }),
    })
    expect(results.stale).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new DoseConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current: results.logged }),
    })
  })

  it("answers NotFound for a dose that was deleted, even from its current version", async () => {
    const result = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql)
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      return editDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2, occurredAt: minutesAgo(5) })(sql)
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: new NotFound() })
  })
})

describe("deleting and restoring a dose", () => {
  it("deletes it softly, as the next version, and invalidates its days and the deleted list", async () => {
    const { logged, result } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql)
      return { logged, result: deleteDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql) }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: { ...logged, version: 2, deletedAt: NOW },
      changed: [...CHANGED_TODAY, ["deleted"]],
    })
  })

  it("restores it as it was, even onto a date that has another dose since, without asking", async () => {
    const { logged, result } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql, minutesAgo(120))
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      dosed(sql, minutesAgo(60))
      return { logged, result: restoreDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(2))(sql) }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      response: { ...logged, version: 3 },
      changed: [...CHANGED_TODAY, ["deleted"]],
    })
  })

  it("refuses to restore a dose from before the birth date with before_birth, and leaves it deleted (D10)", async () => {
    const { result, deletedAt } = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql, SEP_3_BEGINS)
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      movedBirthDate(sql, "2026-09-04")
      return {
        result: restoreDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(2))(sql),
        deletedAt: sql.exec<{ deleted_at: number }>("SELECT deleted_at FROM supplement_logs").one().deleted_at,
      }
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "occurredAt", issue: "before_birth" }] }),
    })
    expect(deletedAt).toBe(NOW)
  })

  it("answers NotFound when restoring a dose that isn't deleted", async () => {
    const result = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql)
      return restoreDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql)
    })
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: new NotFound() })
  })
})

describe("a dose and the household's settings", () => {
  it("keeps the birth date from moving past a dose's date, but not past a deleted dose's", async () => {
    const results = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      const logged = dosed(sql, SEP_3_BEGINS)
      const later = { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, ...SEED.child, birthDate: "2026-09-04" }
      const refused = editChild(stampOf(sql), later)(sql)
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), logged.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      return { refused, allowed: editChild(stampOf(sql), later)(sql).ok }
    })
    expect(results.refused).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "birthDate", issue: "before_birth" }] }),
    })
    expect(results.allowed).toBe(true)
  })

  it("files every dose, deleted ones included, under its date in a new time zone, in the zone change's own save (D8)", async () => {
    const dates = await inHousehold((sql) => {
      dosed(sql, SEP_30_AT_23_30)
      const deleted = dosed(sql, minutesAgo(60))
      saved(deleteDose(stampOf(sql), deleted.id, versioned(1))(sql))
      const units = { volume: "ml", weight: "kg", length: "cm" } as const
      saved(editHousehold(stampOf(sql), { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, timeZone: "Asia/Tokyo", units })(sql))
      return sql
        .exec<{ local_date: string; version: number }>("SELECT local_date, version FROM supplement_logs ORDER BY rowid")
        .toArray()
    })
    expect(dates).toEqual([
      { local_date: "2026-10-01", version: 1 },
      { local_date: "2026-10-09", version: 2 },
    ])
  })
})
````

- [ ] **Step 3: The writes.** `logDose` copies the supplement's dose and asks about a second dose that day (P5, P6). A move that keeps the time skips the time checks. A restore re-runs them (P8).

````ts
import {
  type Dose,
  DoseConflict,
  type DoseEdit,
  InvalidInput,
  type NewDose,
  NotFound,
  queryKeys,
  type VersionedAction,
} from "@bt/contracts"
import { type Instant, localDateOf, type TimeZone } from "@bt/domain"
import { type Calendar, calendarOf, changedKeys, timeIssue } from "../calendar.ts"
import type { CoreResult, Stamp } from "../receipts.ts"
import { DOSE_COLUMNS, type DoseRow, doseOf } from "./doses.ts"

type Found =
  | { readonly ok: true; readonly row: DoseRow }
  | { readonly ok: false; readonly error: NotFound | DoseConflict }

const moment = (at: Instant | null) => (at === null ? null : { start: at, end: at })

const invalidTime = (calendar: Calendar, occurredAt: Instant, now: Instant): InvalidInput | null => {
  const issue = timeIssue(calendar, occurredAt, now)
  return issue === null ? null : new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "occurredAt", issue }] })
}

const doseFor = (sql: SqlStorage, id: string, version: number, state: "live" | "deleted"): Found => {
  const [row] = sql.exec<DoseRow>(`SELECT ${DOSE_COLUMNS} FROM supplement_logs WHERE id = ?`, id).toArray()
  if (row === undefined) return { ok: false, error: new NotFound() }
  if (row.version !== version) {
    return { ok: false, error: new DoseConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current: doseOf(row) }) }
  }
  if ((row.deleted_at === null ? "live" : "deleted") !== state) return { ok: false, error: new NotFound() }
  return { ok: true, row }
}

const doseThatDay = (sql: SqlStorage, supplementId: string, localDate: string): DoseRow | undefined =>
  sql
    .exec<DoseRow>(
      `SELECT ${DOSE_COLUMNS} FROM supplement_logs
       WHERE local_date = ? AND supplement_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY occurred_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1`,
      localDate,
      supplementId,
    )
    .toArray()[0]

const supplementDose = (sql: SqlStorage, id: string): string | undefined =>
  sql.exec<{ dose: string }>("SELECT dose FROM supplements WHERE id = ?", id).toArray()[0]?.dose

export const logDose =
  (stamp: Stamp, entry: NewDose) =>
  (sql: SqlStorage): CoreResult<Dose, NotFound | InvalidInput | DoseConflict> => {
    const dose = supplementDose(sql, entry.supplementId)
    if (dose === undefined) return { ok: false, error: new NotFound() }
    const calendar = calendarOf(sql)
    const invalid = invalidTime(calendar, entry.occurredAt, stamp.now)
    if (invalid !== null) return { ok: false, error: invalid }
    const localDate = localDateOf(entry.occurredAt, calendar.zone)
    const already = entry.confirmedDuplicate ? undefined : doseThatDay(sql, entry.supplementId, localDate)
    if (already !== undefined) {
      return { ok: false, error: new DoseConflict({ code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current: doseOf(already) }) }
    }
    const row = sql
      .exec<DoseRow>(
        `INSERT INTO supplement_logs
           (id, supplement_id, occurred_at, local_date, dose, version, created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING ${DOSE_COLUMNS}`,
        crypto.randomUUID(),
        entry.supplementId,
        entry.occurredAt,
        localDate,
        dose,
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
      )
      .one()
    return { ok: true, response: doseOf(row), changed: changedKeys(calendar, null, moment(row.occurred_at)) }
  }

export const editDose =
  (stamp: Stamp, id: string, edit: DoseEdit) =>
  (sql: SqlStorage): CoreResult<Dose, NotFound | InvalidInput | DoseConflict> => {
    const found = doseFor(sql, id, edit.version, "live")
    if (!found.ok) return found
    const calendar = calendarOf(sql)
    const kept = edit.occurredAt === found.row.occurred_at
    const invalid = kept ? null : invalidTime(calendar, edit.occurredAt, stamp.now)
    if (invalid !== null) return { ok: false, error: invalid }
    const row = sql
      .exec<DoseRow>(
        `UPDATE supplement_logs SET occurred_at = ?, local_date = ?, version = version + 1, updated_by = ?, updated_at = ?
         WHERE id = ? RETURNING ${DOSE_COLUMNS}`,
        edit.occurredAt,
        localDateOf(edit.occurredAt, calendar.zone),
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
        id,
      )
      .one()
    const changed = changedKeys(calendar, moment(found.row.occurred_at), moment(row.occurred_at))
    return { ok: true, response: doseOf(row), changed }
  }

const setDeletedAt =
  (stamp: Stamp, id: string, action: VersionedAction, deletedAt: Instant | null) =>
  (sql: SqlStorage): CoreResult<Dose, NotFound | InvalidInput | DoseConflict> => {
    const found = doseFor(sql, id, action.version, deletedAt === null ? "deleted" : "live")
    if (!found.ok) return found
    const calendar = calendarOf(sql)
    const invalid = deletedAt === null ? invalidTime(calendar, found.row.occurred_at, stamp.now) : null
    if (invalid !== null) return { ok: false, error: invalid }
    const row = sql
      .exec<DoseRow>(
        `UPDATE supplement_logs SET deleted_at = ?, version = version + 1, updated_by = ?, updated_at = ?
         WHERE id = ? RETURNING ${DOSE_COLUMNS}`,
        deletedAt,
        stamp.caregiverId,
        stamp.now,
        id,
      )
      .one()
    return {
      ok: true,
      response: doseOf(row),
      changed: [...changedKeys(calendar, moment(row.occurred_at), null), queryKeys.deleted],
    }
  }

export const deleteDose = (stamp: Stamp, id: string, action: VersionedAction) =>
  setDeletedAt(stamp, id, action, stamp.now)

export const restoreDose = (stamp: Stamp, id: string, action: VersionedAction) => setDeletedAt(stamp, id, action, null)

export const refileDoses = (sql: SqlStorage, zone: TimeZone) => {
  const rows = sql.exec<{ id: string; occurred_at: number }>("SELECT id, occurred_at FROM supplement_logs").toArray()
  for (const { id, occurred_at } of rows) {
    sql.exec("UPDATE supplement_logs SET local_date = ? WHERE id = ?", localDateOf(occurred_at, zone), id)
  }
}
````

A zone change refiles every dose in its own save, and the birth date can't move past a live dose:

````diff
          UNION ALL SELECT min(started_at) FROM feeds WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
          UNION ALL SELECT min(measured_at) FROM growth_measurements WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
          UNION ALL SELECT min(started_at) FROM sleeps WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
````
