- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

````ts
import { Me, SavedDose } from "@bt/contracts"
import { Schema } from "effect"
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest"
import { ANDREW, CHANGED_TODAY, EMILY, HOUR_MS, MINUTE_MS, NOW, seededHousehold } from "./fixtures.ts"
import { type Household, get, inStorage, send, sendJson } from "./requests.ts"

const decodeSaved = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(Schema.toCodecJson(SavedDose))
const decodeMe = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(Schema.toCodecJson(Me))

const vitaminDOf = async (household: Household) => {
  const [vitaminD] = decodeMe(await (await get(household, "/api/me", ANDREW.email)).json()).household.supplements
  if (vitaminD === undefined) throw new Error("expected the seeded Vitamin D")
  return vitaminD.id
}

const doseAt = (supplementId: string, occurredAt: number, confirmedDuplicate = false) => ({
  actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
  supplementId,
  occurredAt,
  confirmedDuplicate,
})

const logged = async (household: Household, occurredAt = NOW - HOUR_MS) =>
  decodeSaved((await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt(await vitaminDOf(household), occurredAt))).body)
    .dose

const caregiverId = (household: Household, email: string) =>
  inStorage(household, (sql) => sql.exec<{ id: string }>("SELECT id FROM caregivers WHERE email = ?", email).one().id)

const doseRows = (household: Household) =>
  inStorage(household, (sql) => sql.exec("SELECT id FROM supplement_logs").toArray())

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] })
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW)
})

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
})

describe("POST /api/doses", () => {
  it("logs a dose and answers 201, no-store, with it and the keys it changed", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const vitaminD = await vitaminDOf(household)
    const response = await send(household, "POST", "/api/doses", JSON.stringify(doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - HOUR_MS)))
    const andrew = await caregiverId(household, ANDREW.email)
    expect(response.status).toBe(201)
    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store")
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({
      dose: {
        id: expect.any(String),
        supplementId: vitaminD,
        occurredAt: NOW - HOUR_MS,
        localDate: "2026-10-08",
        dose: "400 IU",
        version: 1,
        createdBy: andrew,
        createdAt: NOW,
        updatedBy: andrew,
        updatedAt: NOW,
        deletedAt: null,
      },
      changed: CHANGED_TODAY,
    })
  })

  it("answers 409 SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY with the day's dose for a second one, then logs it once confirmed", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const first = await logged(household, NOW - 3 * HOUR_MS)
    const vitaminD = await vitaminDOf(household)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - MINUTE_MS), EMILY.email)).toEqual({
      status: 409,
      body: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current: first },
    })
    const confirmed = await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - MINUTE_MS, true))
    expect(confirmed.status).toBe(201)
    expect(await doseRows(household)).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it("lets only one of two phones' doses that day through unasked when both log at the same moment", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const vitaminD = await vitaminDOf(household)
    const statuses = await Promise.all([
      sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - MINUTE_MS), ANDREW.email),
      sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - MINUTE_MS), EMILY.email),
    ])
    expect(statuses.map(({ status }) => status).toSorted()).toEqual([201, 409])
    expect(await doseRows(household)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it("answers a retry of a save whose answer was lost with the stored 201, and keeps one dose (AC 14)", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const dose = doseAt(await vitaminDOf(household), NOW - HOUR_MS)
    const first = await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", dose)
    vi.setSystemTime(NOW + 30_000)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", dose)).toEqual(first)
    expect(await doseRows(household)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it("answers a retry with another time with 409 ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH and the saved dose", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const dose = doseAt(await vitaminDOf(household), NOW - HOUR_MS)
    const first = decodeSaved((await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", dose)).body)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", { ...dose, occurredAt: NOW - 2 * HOUR_MS })).toEqual({
      status: 409,
      body: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH", current: first.dose },
    })
  })

  it("answers 404 for a supplement the household doesn't hold, and 400 naming each bad field", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", doseAt("no-such-supplement", NOW))).toEqual({
      status: 404,
      body: { _tag: "NotFound" },
    })
    const bad = { ...doseAt(await vitaminDOf(household), NOW), occurredAt: "now", confirmedDuplicate: "no" }
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", "/api/doses", bad)).toEqual({
      status: 400,
      body: {
        _tag: "InvalidInput",
        fieldErrors: [
          { path: "occurredAt", issue: "invalid" },
          { path: "confirmedDuplicate", issue: "invalid" },
        ],
      },
    })
  })
})

describe("PATCH, DELETE and POST …/restore", () => {
  it("move, delete and restore a dose, each answering with it and the keys it changed", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const dose = await logged(household)
    const path = `/api/doses/${dose.id}`
    const emily = await caregiverId(household, EMILY.email)
    const moved = { ...dose, occurredAt: NOW - 2 * HOUR_MS, version: 2, updatedBy: emily }
    const edit = { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 1, occurredAt: NOW - 2 * HOUR_MS }
    expect(await sendJson(household, "PATCH", path, edit, EMILY.email)).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: { dose: moved, changed: CHANGED_TODAY },
    })
    const deleted = { ...moved, version: 3, updatedBy: dose.createdBy, deletedAt: NOW }
    expect(await sendJson(household, "DELETE", path, { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 2 })).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: { dose: deleted, changed: [...CHANGED_TODAY, ["deleted"]] },
    })
    expect(await sendJson(household, "POST", `${path}/restore`, { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 3 })).toEqual(
      {
        status: 200,
        body: { dose: { ...deleted, version: 4, deletedAt: null }, changed: [...CHANGED_TODAY, ["deleted"]] },
      },
    )
  })

  it("answers 409 STALE_VERSION with the dose as it is now for a move from an old version", async () => {
    const household = await seededHousehold()
    const dose = await logged(household)
    const move = (occurredAt: number) => ({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 1, occurredAt })
    const first = decodeSaved((await sendJson(household, "PATCH", `/api/doses/${dose.id}`, move(NOW - MINUTE_MS))).body)
    expect(await sendJson(household, "PATCH", `/api/doses/${dose.id}`, move(NOW), EMILY.email)).toEqual({
      status: 409,
      body: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: first.dose },
    })
  })

  it("answers 404 for a dose the household doesn't hold", async () => {
    const response = await sendJson(await seededHousehold(), "DELETE", "/api/doses/no-such-dose", {
      actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
      version: 1,
    })
    expect(response).toEqual({ status: 404, body: { _tag: "NotFound" } })
  })
})
````

````diff
 export type DoseRow = {

@@ -32,12 +32,22 @@ export const doseOf = (row: DoseRow): Dose => ({

+export const liveDosesSince = (sql: SqlStorage, from: Instant): Dose[] =>

+
+export const liveDosesIn = (sql: SqlStorage, period: Interval): Dose[] =>
+  dosesWhere(

+    "WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND occurred_at >= ? AND occurred_at < ? ORDER BY occurred_at, rowid",
+    period.start,
+    period.end,
+  )
+
+export const deletedDosesSince = (sql: SqlStorage, since: Instant): Dose[] =>
+  dosesWhere(sql, "WHERE deleted_at >= ? ORDER BY deleted_at DESC, rowid DESC", since)
````

````diff
+    const response = await send(household, "POST", "/api/doses", JSON.stringify(dose))
+    const body = await response.text()
+    times.push(performance.now() - begun)
+    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`POST /api/doses answered ${response.status}: ${body}`)
+  }
````

### Task 6: Doses reach the web's data layer

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/api/supplements.ts`, `apps/web/src/api/today-doses.ts`, `apps/web/test/dose-cache.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/supplements-save.test.ts`
- Modify:
  - `packages/contracts/src/today.ts` (`RecentEntry`), `apps/worker/src/core/today.ts`
  - the web's `api/`: `actions.ts`, `send.ts`, `pending.ts`, `today-change.ts`, `today-cache.ts`, `swap.ts`, `entry-cache.ts`, `optimistic.ts`, `hooks.ts`
  - the web's `sheets/`: `sending.ts`, `sheets-state.ts`, `use-reopen.ts`
  - the web's features: `edit/stale.ts`, `today/display.ts`, `today/kind-icons.ts`, `today/recent-rows.ts`, and History's `day-model.ts`, `day-grid.tsx`, `entry-details.ts`, `entry-labels.ts`, `grid-markers.ts`
  - `docs/api.json` (generated)
- Test:
  - `packages/contracts/test/today.test.ts`, `apps/worker/test/today-core.test.ts`
  - `apps/web/test/send.test.ts`, `sheets-state.test.ts`, `today-rows.test.ts`, `history-labels.test.ts`, `entry-swaps.test.ts`, `optimistic.test.ts`, `today-cache.test.ts`, `today-withdrawal.test.ts`, `diaper-edit.test.ts`, `today-fixtures.ts`, `history-fixtures.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `Today.doses`, the periods' `doses` and the fixtures' `dosedAt`; Task 1's requests; Task 2's `Me.household.supplements`.
- Produces:
  - contracts' `RecentEntry` gains `{ kind: "dose"; dose: Dose }`. The Worker lists live doses of the last 24 hours in `recent`, and reads every dose since the earlier of today's start and 24 hours ago once.
  - `api/actions.ts`: `DoseAction` (`logDose`, `editDose`, `deleteDose`, `restoreDose`), which joins `Action`, and `isDoseAction(action)`
  - `api/send.ts`: `Saved` includes `SavedDose`
  - `api/pending.ts`: `pendingDose(payload: NewDose, author, { localDate, dose }): Dose`
  - `api/today-change.ts`:
    - `Pending` gains `supplements: ReadonlyArray<Supplement>`, and `pendingOf(me, now): Pending` builds it
    - `idOf`, `timeOf` and `versionOf` take a dose
    - `changeOf(dose)` is `NOTHING`
  - `api/today-doses.ts`: `doseTextOf(supplements, supplementId)`, `withoutTodaysDose(today, id)`, and `withTodaysDose(today, dose)`, which keeps today's live doses in time order
  - `api/swap.ts`: `Swapping` gains `editDose` and `deleteDose`; `swappedEntry`, `swappedToday`, `swappedDay`, `todayCopy` and `dayCopy` take doses
  - `api/supplements.ts`:
    - `SupplementFields { name, dose, stopped }`
    - `saveSupplement(api, shown: Supplement | null, fields): Promise<SavedSupplement>`, which throws `InvalidInput` before sending a bad field (P4)
    - `withSavedSupplement(me, supplement): Me`
  - `api/hooks.ts`: `useSaveSupplement()` and `SaveSupplement`
  - `sheets/sending.ts`: `SheetKind` gains `"dose"` and `"editDose"`. `logDose` and `deleteDose` close their sheet on send, and `editDose` edits a record.
  - `NEW_ENTRIES` gains `logDose`, and `TODAY_SHEETS` gains `"dose"`.
  - `features/edit/stale.ts`: `staleOf` and `recordIn` know `DoseConflict` and a dose
  - `features/today/display.ts`: `Display` gains `supplements`, and `supplementName(display, id)` falls back to "Supplement"
  - `features/today/kind-icons.ts`: `KIND_ICONS.dose = PillIcon`, and `RowKind` gains `"dose"`
  - `features/today/recent-rows.ts`: a dose row titled by its supplement, with its dose. It opens nothing yet; Task 8 opens "editDose".
  - History:
    - `Entry` gains `{ kind: "dose"; dose }`, and `editSheetOf(entry)` may answer `"editDose"`
    - `entryDetails` shows a dose
    - `markerText(entry, display, now)` now takes the `Display`, not a volume unit
    - `entryName` names "Vitamin D at 9:00 AM: 400 IU"
    - `MarkerKind` gains `"dose"`, and `MARKER_ICONS.dose` is `PillIcon`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** Doses in the optimistic layer, new:

````ts
import { type DayPeriod, type Me, queryKeys, type Today } from "@bt/contracts"
import { zonedDayBounds } from "@bt/domain"
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import type { Action } from "../src/api/actions.ts"
import { cachedEntry } from "../src/api/entry-cache.ts"
import { pendIn, rollBack, settle } from "../src/api/optimistic.ts"
import { pendingId } from "../src/api/pending.ts"
import { pendingToday, savedToday, withdrawnToday } from "../src/api/today-cache.ts"
import { meWith } from "./growth-fixtures.ts"
import { dayWith, local, NOW_ON_DAY } from "./history-fixtures.ts"
import {
  ANDREW,
  diaperAt,
  dosedAt,
  MINUTE,
  NO_TOTALS,
  supplementOf,
  todayWith,
  VITAMIN_D,
  ZONE,
} from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const VITAMIN_D_SUPPLEMENT = supplementOf()
const PENDING = { by: ANDREW, now: NOW_ON_DAY, zone: ZONE, supplements: [VITAMIN_D_SUPPLEMENT] }
const YESTERDAY_AT_23_30 = local("23:30", "2026-10-08")

const logAt = (occurredAt: number): Action => ({
  kind: "logDose",
  payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, supplementId: VITAMIN_D, occurredAt, confirmedDuplicate: false },
})

const pendingDoseAt = (occurredAt: number, localDate: string) => ({
  id: pendingId(ACTION_ID),
  supplementId: VITAMIN_D,
  occurredAt,
  localDate,
  dose: "400 IU",
  version: 1,
  createdBy: ANDREW,
  createdAt: NOW_ON_DAY,
  updatedBy: ANDREW,
  updatedAt: NOW_ON_DAY,
  deletedAt: null,
})

describe("a dose being logged", () => {
  it("lists it first in the last 24 hours and in today's doses, at its supplement's dose, counting nothing", () => {
    const earlier = diaperAt(NOW_ON_DAY - 60 * MINUTE)
    const today = todayWith({ recent: [{ kind: "diaper", diaper: earlier }] })
    const next = pendingToday(today, logAt(NOW_ON_DAY - 5 * MINUTE), PENDING)
    const dose = pendingDoseAt(NOW_ON_DAY - 5 * MINUTE, "2026-10-09")
    expect(next).toEqual({
      ...today,
      recent: [
        { kind: "dose", dose },
        { kind: "diaper", diaper: earlier },
      ],
      doses: [dose],
    })
    expect(next.totals).toEqual(NO_TOTALS)
  })

  it("leaves today's doses alone for a dose backdated to yesterday, though it's in the last 24 hours", () => {
    const next = pendingToday(todayWith(), logAt(YESTERDAY_AT_23_30), PENDING)
    expect(next.recent).toEqual([{ kind: "dose", dose: pendingDoseAt(YESTERDAY_AT_23_30, "2026-10-08") }])
    expect(next.doses).toEqual([])
  })

  it("takes the dose back off both lists when its save fails, leaving the rest", () => {
    const kept = dosedAt(local("07:00"))
    const before = todayWith({ recent: [{ kind: "dose", dose: kept }], doses: [kept] })
    const shown = pendingToday(before, logAt(NOW_ON_DAY), PENDING)
    expect(withdrawnToday(shown, logAt(NOW_ON_DAY), PENDING, before)).toEqual(before)
  })

  it("swaps the pending dose for the household's answer in both lists", () => {
    const shown = pendingToday(todayWith(), logAt(NOW_ON_DAY), PENDING)
    const saved = dosedAt(NOW_ON_DAY, { id: "dose-saved", createdBy: ANDREW, updatedBy: ANDREW })
    const next = savedToday(shown, { dose: saved, changed: [] }, logAt(NOW_ON_DAY), NOW_ON_DAY)
    expect(next.recent).toEqual([{ kind: "dose", dose: saved }])
    expect(next.doses).toEqual([saved])
  })

  it("shows a restored dose in today's doses once the household answers, in time order", () => {
    const later = dosedAt(local("08:00"))
    const restored = dosedAt(local("07:00"), { id: "dose-restored", version: 3 })
    const action: Action = { kind: "restoreDose", id: restored.id, payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 2 } }
    const today = todayWith({ recent: [{ kind: "dose", dose: later }], doses: [later] })
    const next = savedToday(today, { dose: restored, changed: [] }, action, NOW_ON_DAY)
    expect(next.doses).toEqual([restored, later])
    expect(next.recent).toEqual([
      { kind: "dose", dose: later },
      { kind: "dose", dose: restored },
    ])
  })
})

describe("an edit or delete of a dose", () => {
  const DOSE = dosedAt(local("07:00"), { id: "dose-1" })
  const ME: Me = { ...meWith(), household: { ...meWith().household, supplements: [VITAMIN_D_SUPPLEMENT] } }

  const householdCache = () => {
    const queryClient = new QueryClient()
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, ME)
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.today, todayWith({ recent: [{ kind: "dose", dose: DOSE }], doses: [DOSE] }))
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09"), dayWith({ feeds: [], diapers: [], doses: [DOSE] }))
    queryClient.setQueryData(
      queryKeys.day("2026-10-08"),
      dayWith({ date: "2026-10-08", bounds: zonedDayBounds("2026-10-08", ZONE), feeds: [], diapers: [] }),
    )
    return queryClient
  }

  const MOVE_TO_YESTERDAY: Action = {
    kind: "editDose",
    id: DOSE.id,
    payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: YESTERDAY_AT_23_30 },
  }

  it("moves a dose to its new date on Today and both cached days at once, and puts them back when it fails", async () => {
    const queryClient = householdCache()
    const before = {
      today: queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today),
      day: queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09")),
      yesterday: queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day("2026-10-08")),
    }
    const pended = await pendIn(queryClient, MOVE_TO_YESTERDAY, NOW_ON_DAY)
    const moved = {
      ...DOSE,
      occurredAt: YESTERDAY_AT_23_30,
      localDate: "2026-10-08",
      updatedBy: ANDREW,
      updatedAt: NOW_ON_DAY,
    }
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)).toMatchObject({
      recent: [{ kind: "dose", dose: moved }],
      doses: [],
    })
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09"))?.doses).toEqual([])
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day("2026-10-08"))?.doses).toEqual([moved])
    rollBack(queryClient, MOVE_TO_YESTERDAY, pended)
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.today)).toEqual(before.today)
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09"))).toEqual(before.day)
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData(queryKeys.day("2026-10-08"))).toEqual(before.yesterday)
  })

  it("takes a deleted dose off Today and its day at once, and keeps it off once the household answers", async () => {
    const queryClient = householdCache()
    const action: Action = { kind: "deleteDose", id: DOSE.id, payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1 } }
    await pendIn(queryClient, action, NOW_ON_DAY)
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)).toMatchObject({ recent: [], doses: [] })
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<DayPeriod>(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09"))?.doses).toEqual([])
    settle(queryClient, { dose: { ...DOSE, version: 2, deletedAt: NOW_ON_DAY }, changed: [] }, action, NOW_ON_DAY)
    expect(queryClient.getQueryData<Today>(queryKeys.today)).toMatchObject({ recent: [], doses: [] })
  })

  it("opens an edit sheet on the newest copy of a dose, from Today's doses or a cached day", () => {
    const queryClient = householdCache()
    const newer = { ...DOSE, version: 2, occurredAt: local("07:10") }
    queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.day("2026-10-09"), dayWith({ feeds: [], diapers: [], doses: [newer] }))
    expect(cachedEntry(queryClient, DOSE.id)).toEqual({ kind: "dose", dose: newer })
  })
})
````

A supplement's save, new:

````ts
import { InvalidInput, type Me } from "@bt/contracts"
import { Schema } from "effect"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { connectApi } from "../src/api/client.ts"
import { saveSupplement, withSavedSupplement } from "../src/api/supplements.ts"
import { meWith } from "./growth-fixtures.ts"
import { supplementOf } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ORIGIN = "https://bt.example.test"
const VITAMIN_D = supplementOf()
const IRON = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-iron", name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL", createdBy: "caregiver-andrew" })

const json = (status: number, body: Schema.Json) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } })

const householdAnswering = (...answers: Response[]) => {
  const sent: Array<{ readonly method: string; readonly path: string; readonly body: Schema.Json }> = []
  const api = connectApi({
    baseUrl: ORIGIN,
    report: () => undefined,
    fetch: async (input, init) => {
      const request = new Request(input, init)
      sent.push({ method: request.method, path: new URL(request.url).pathname, body: await request.json() })
      return answers.shift() ?? json(500, {})
    },
  })
  return { api, sent }
}

describe("saveSupplement", () => {
  it("adds a supplement with its name and dose at /api/supplements", async () => {
    const { api, sent } = householdAnswering(json(201, { supplement: IRON, changed: [["me"]] }))
    await expect(saveSupplement(api, null, { name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL", stopped: false })).resolves.toEqual({
      supplement: IRON,
      changed: [["me"]],
    })
    expect(sent).toEqual([
      { method: "POST", path: "/api/supplements", body: { actionId: expect.any(String), name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" } },
    ])
  })

  it("saves a new name, dose or stop at /api/supplements/:id from the version shown", async () => {
    const stopped = { ...VITAMIN_D, name: "Vitamin D3", stoppedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9), version: 2 }
    const { api, sent } = householdAnswering(json(200, { supplement: stopped, changed: [["me"]] }))
    await saveSupplement(api, VITAMIN_D, { name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "400 IU", stopped: true })
    expect(sent).toEqual([
      {
        method: "PATCH",
        path: `/api/supplements/${VITAMIN_D.id}`,
        body: { actionId: expect.any(String), version: 1, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "400 IU", stopped: true },
      },
    ])
  })

  it("puts its change on top of the other phone's newer copy once, keeping what that phone changed and this one didn't", async () => {
    const theirs = { ...VITAMIN_D, dose: "800 IU", version: 2 }
    const saved = { ...theirs, name: "Vitamin D3", version: 3 }
    const { api, sent } = householdAnswering(
      json(409, { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: theirs }),
      json(200, { supplement: saved, changed: [["me"]] }),
    )
    await expect(
      saveSupplement(api, VITAMIN_D, { name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "400 IU", stopped: false }),
    ).resolves.toMatchObject({ supplement: saved })
    expect(sent.map(({ body }) => body)).toEqual([
      { actionId: expect.any(String), version: 1, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "400 IU", stopped: false },
      { actionId: expect.any(String), version: 2, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "800 IU", stopped: false },
    ])
  })

  it("keeps the other phone's new name and stop when this phone changed only the dose", async () => {
    const theirs = { ...VITAMIN_D, name: "Vitamin D3", stoppedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9), version: 2 }
    const saved = { ...theirs, dose: "600 IU", version: 3 }
    const { api, sent } = householdAnswering(
      json(409, { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: theirs }),
      json(200, { supplement: saved, changed: [["me"]] }),
    )
    await saveSupplement(api, VITAMIN_D, { name: "Vitamin D", dose: "600 IU", stopped: false })
    expect(sent.map(({ body }) => body).at(-1)).toEqual({
      actionId: expect.any(String),
      version: 2,
      name: "Vitamin D3",
      dose: "600 IU",
      stopped: true,
    })
  })

  it("refuses a blank name and a dose over 40 characters before sending, naming each field", async () => {
    const { api, sent } = householdAnswering()
    await expect(saveSupplement(api, null, { name: "", dose: "x".repeat(41), stopped: false })).rejects.toEqual(
      new InvalidInput({
        fieldErrors: [
          { path: "name", issue: "invalid" },
          { path: "dose", issue: "invalid" },
        ],
      }),
    )
    expect(sent).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe("withSavedSupplement", () => {
  it("shows a saved supplement in its place, and a new one after the others", () => {
    const me: Me = { ...meWith(), household: { ...meWith().household, supplements: [VITAMIN_D, IRON] } }
    const renamed = { ...VITAMIN_D, name: "Vitamin D3", version: 2 }
    expect(withSavedSupplement(me, renamed).household.supplements).toEqual([renamed, IRON])
    const probiotic = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-probiotic", name: "Probiotic", dose: "" })
    expect(withSavedSupplement(me, probiotic).household.supplements).toEqual([VITAMIN_D, IRON, probiotic])
  })
})
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and watch them fail.**

Run: `pnpm exec vitest run apps/web/test/dose-cache.test.ts apps/web/test/supplements-save.test.ts apps/web/test/send.test.ts apps/web/test/sheets-state.test.ts apps/web/test/today-rows.test.ts apps/web/test/history-labels.test.ts apps/web/test/entry-swaps.test.ts apps/web/test/optimistic.test.ts apps/web/test/today-cache.test.ts apps/web/test/today-withdrawal.test.ts apps/web/test/diaper-edit.test.ts packages/contracts/test/today.test.ts apps/worker/test/today-core.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL in 8 of 13 files: 18 tests, with 229 passing.
- `supplements-save.test.ts` can't load `../src/api/supplements.ts`.
- `RecentEntry` has no dose kind, `send` has no dose route, and nothing closes on `logDose`.
- Today's rows and History's labels don't know a dose.

````diff
+export type Saved = SavedDiaper | SavedFeed | SavedMeasurement | SavedDose

 type NewFeed = Exclude<FeedAction, OnFeed>
@@ -96,7 +98,21 @@ const sendMeasurement = (api: HouseholdApi, action: MeasurementAction): Promise<
   }

+const sendDose = (api: HouseholdApi, action: DoseAction): Promise<SavedDose> => {
+  switch (action.kind) {
+    case "logDose":
+      return api.request((client) => client.doses.logDose({ payload: action.payload }))
+    case "editDose":
+      return api.request((client) => client.doses.editDose({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+    case "deleteDose":
+      return api.request((client) => client.doses.deleteDose({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+    case "restoreDose":
+      return api.request((client) => client.doses.restoreDose({ params: { id: action.id }, payload: action.payload }))
+  }
+}
+
 export const send = (api: HouseholdApi, action: Action): Promise<Saved> => {
+  if (isDoseAction(action)) return sendDose(api, action)
   if (isMeasurementAction(action)) return sendMeasurement(api, action)
   if (isDiaperAction(action)) return sendDiaper(api, action)
   return "id" in action ? sendOnFeed(api, action) : sendNewFeed(api, action)
````

````ts
import type { Dose, Supplement, Today } from "@bt/contracts"

export const doseTextOf = (supplements: ReadonlyArray<Supplement>, supplementId: string) =>
  supplements.find(({ id }) => id === supplementId)?.dose ?? ""

export const withoutTodaysDose = (today: Today, id: string): Today => ({
  ...today,
  doses: today.doses.filter((dose) => dose.id !== id),
})

export const withTodaysDose = (today: Today, dose: Dose): Today => {
  const kept = withoutTodaysDose(today, dose.id)
  if (dose.deletedAt !== null || dose.localDate !== today.date) return kept
  return { ...kept, doses: [...kept.doses, dose].toSorted((a, b) => a.occurredAt - b.occurredAt) }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 6: A supplement's save.**

````ts
import {
  invalidInputFrom,
  type Me,
  NewSupplement,
  type SavedSupplement,
  type Supplement,
  SupplementConflict,
  SupplementEdit,
} from "@bt/contracts"
import { Result, Schema } from "effect"
import type { HouseholdApi } from "./client.ts"

export interface SupplementFields {
  readonly name: string
  readonly dose: string
  readonly stopped: boolean
}

const checkNew = Schema.decodeUnknownResult(NewSupplement, { errors: "all" })
const checkEdit = Schema.decodeUnknownResult(SupplementEdit, { errors: "all" })

const isStopped = (supplement: Supplement) => supplement.stoppedAt !== null

const supplementEdit = (latest: Supplement, shown: Supplement, fields: SupplementFields) => {
  const edit = checkEdit({
    actionId: crypto.randomUUID(),
    version: latest.version,
    name: fields.name === shown.name ? latest.name : fields.name,
    dose: fields.dose === shown.dose ? latest.dose : fields.dose,
    stopped: fields.stopped === isStopped(shown) ? isStopped(latest) : fields.stopped,
  })
  if (Result.isFailure(edit)) throw invalidInputFrom(edit.failure.issue)
  return edit.success
}

const addSupplement = (api: HouseholdApi, { name, dose }: SupplementFields) => {
  const entry = checkNew({ actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), name, dose })
  if (Result.isFailure(entry)) throw invalidInputFrom(entry.failure.issue)
  return api.request((client) => client.supplements.addSupplement({ payload: entry.success }))
}

export const saveSupplement = async (
  api: HouseholdApi,
  shown: Supplement | null,
  fields: SupplementFields,
): Promise<SavedSupplement> => {
  if (shown === null) return addSupplement(api, fields)
  const send = (latest: Supplement) => {
    const payload = supplementEdit(latest, shown, fields)
    return api.request((client) => client.supplements.editSupplement({ params: { id: shown.id }, payload }))
  }
  try {
    return await send(shown)
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof SupplementConflict && error.code === "STALE_VERSION") return send(error.current)
    throw error
  }
}

export const withSavedSupplement = (me: Me, supplement: Supplement): Me => {
  const listed = me.household.supplements
  const supplements = listed.some(({ id }) => id === supplement.id)
    ? listed.map((one) => (one.id === supplement.id ? supplement : one))
    : [...listed, supplement]
  return { ...me, household: { ...me.household, supplements } }
}
````

````diff
+export const useSaveSupplement = () => {

+  return useMutation({
+    mutationFn: ({ shown, fields }: { readonly shown: Supplement | null; readonly fields: SupplementFields }) =>
+      saveSupplement(api, shown, fields),
+    onSuccess: ({ supplement, changed }) =>
+      rememberSaved(queryClient, (me) => withSavedSupplement(me, supplement), changed),
+  })
+}
+
+export type SaveSupplement = ReturnType<typeof useSaveSupplement>
````

````diff
+    case "dose":
+      return supplementName(display, entry.dose.supplementId)
+    case "feed":
+      return formatVolume(feedSummary(entry.feed, now).bottleMl, display.volume)
+  }
+}
+
+const doseName = (dose: Dose, display: Display, time: string) => {
+  const name = `${supplementName(display, dose.supplementId)} at ${time}`
+  return dose.dose === "" ? name : `${name}: ${dose.dose}`
+}
 
 export const entryName = (entry: Entry, display: Display, now: number) => {
   const time = (at: number) => clockTime(at, display.timeZone, display.locale)
   if (entry.kind === "diaper") return `Diaper at ${time(entry.diaper.occurredAt)}: ${diaperText(entry.diaper)}`
+  if (entry.kind === "dose") return doseName(entry.dose, display, time(entry.dose.occurredAt))
   const { feed } = entry
   const details = [...progressOf(feed, "in progress"), ...feedParts(feed, display.volume, now)]
   const name = `${feedTitle(feed)} at ${time(feed.startedAt)}`
````

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { pendingId } from "../src/api/pending.ts"
import { supplementRows } from "../src/features/today/supplement-rows.ts"
import { DISPLAY, local } from "./history-fixtures.ts"
import { ANDREW, dosedAt, supplementOf, VITAMIN_D } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const IRON = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-iron", name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" })
const PROBIOTIC = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-probiotic", name: "Probiotic", dose: "" })
const STOPPED = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-stopped", name: "Fluoride", stoppedAt: local("06:00") })
const DISPLAYED = { ...DISPLAY, supplements: [supplementOf(), IRON, PROBIOTIC, STOPPED] }

describe("supplementRows", () => {
  it("gives each supplement in use a row, in the household's order, leaving out one that's stopped", () => {
    const rows = supplementRows(DISPLAYED, [])
    expect(rows.map(({ id }) => id)).toEqual([VITAMIN_D, IRON.id, PROBIOTIC.id])
  })

  it("says a supplement isn't logged yet today, with its dose when one is written down", () => {
    expect(supplementRows(DISPLAYED, []).map(({ title, detail }) => [title, detail])).toEqual([
      ["Vitamin D not logged yet today", ["400 IU"]],
      ["Iron not logged yet today", ["1 mL"]],
      ["Probiotic not logged yet today", []],
    ])
  })

  it("says when today's dose was logged and by whom, with the dose it was given at", () => {
    const [row] = supplementRows(DISPLAYED, [dosedAt(local("09:10"), { dose: "800 IU" })])
    expect(row).toEqual({
      id: VITAMIN_D,
      name: "Vitamin D",
      title: "Vitamin D logged at 9:10 AM",
      detail: ["by Emily", "800 IU"],
    })
  })

  it("counts a second dose and names the last, and says a dose is saving until the household answers", () => {
    const doses = [dosedAt(local("09:10")), dosedAt(local("14:02"), { createdBy: ANDREW })]
    const [row] = supplementRows(DISPLAYED, doses)
    expect(row).toMatchObject({
      title: "Vitamin D logged twice today, last at 2:02 PM",
      detail: ["by Andrew", "400 IU"],
    })
    const saving = [...doses, dosedAt(local("15:00"), { id: pendingId("action-1") })]
    expect(supplementRows(DISPLAYED, saving)[0]).toMatchObject({
      title: "Vitamin D logged 3 times today, last at 3:00 PM",
      detail: ["Saving…", "400 IU"],
    })
  })

  it("names no one for a caregiver the household doesn't list", () => {
    const [row] = supplementRows(DISPLAYED, [dosedAt(local("09:10"), { createdBy: "gone" })])
    expect(row?.detail).toEqual(["400 IU"])
  })

  it("gives no rows when no supplement is in use", () => {
    expect(supplementRows({ ...DISPLAY, supplements: [STOPPED] }, [dosedAt(local("09:10"))])).toEqual([])
  })
})
````

````ts
import { DoseConflict } from "@bt/contracts"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import {
  doseSessionOf,
  duplicateMessage,
  duplicateOf,
  supplementIdOf,
} from "../src/features/supplement/dose-session.ts"
import type { Household } from "../src/sheets/failure.ts"
import type { Failed, Sending } from "../src/sheets/sending.ts"
import { NOW as NOW_WHEN, whenAt } from "../src/sheets/when.ts"
import { ANDREW, dosedAt, EMILY, MINUTE, NOW, VITAMIN_D, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/u

const HOUSEHOLD: Household = {
  caregivers: [
    { id: ANDREW, displayName: "Andrew" },
    { id: EMILY, displayName: "Emily" },
  ],
  timeZone: ZONE,
  childName: "Robin",
  locale: "en-US",
}

const logged = (confirmedDuplicate: boolean): Sending => ({
  sheet: "dose",
  action: {
    kind: "logDose",
    payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, supplementId: VITAMIN_D, occurredAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE, confirmedDuplicate },
  },
})

const failedWith = (error: Error, confirmedDuplicate = false): Failed => ({
  sending: logged(confirmedDuplicate),
  error,
})

describe("doseSessionOf", () => {
  it("logs a new dose of the supplement the sheet opened on, now, asking first about a second dose that day", () => {
    expect(doseSessionOf(null, "supplement-iron", ZONE, null)).toEqual({
      actionId: expect.stringMatching(UUID),
      supplementId: "supplement-iron",
      when: NOW_WHEN,
      confirmedDuplicate: false,
    })
  })

  it("starts at the time History's empty slot was tapped", () => {
    expect(doseSessionOf(null, VITAMIN_D, ZONE, NOW - 60 * MINUTE).when).toEqual(whenAt(NOW - 60 * MINUTE, ZONE))
  })

  it("reopens on a dose that wasn't saved with its own action, supplement, time and answer to the second-dose question", () => {
    expect(doseSessionOf(failedWith(new Offline(), true), null, ZONE, null)).toEqual({
      actionId: ACTION_ID,
      supplementId: VITAMIN_D,
      when: whenAt(NOW - 5 * MINUTE, ZONE),
      confirmedDuplicate: true,
    })
  })
})

describe("supplementIdOf", () => {
  it("is the held dose's supplement when one is held, and the opening's otherwise", () => {
    expect(supplementIdOf(failedWith(new Offline()), "supplement-iron")).toBe(VITAMIN_D)
    expect(supplementIdOf(null, "supplement-iron")).toBe("supplement-iron")
    expect(supplementIdOf(null, null)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe("duplicateOf", () => {
  it("finds a second dose that day, with the one already logged and the same dose resent as meant", () => {
    const current = dosedAt(NOW - 180 * MINUTE)
    const failed = failedWith(new DoseConflict({ code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current }))
    const duplicate = duplicateOf(failed)
    expect(duplicate).toMatchObject({ failed, current })
    expect(duplicate?.resend()).toEqual({
      sheet: "dose",
      action: {
        kind: "logDose",
        payload: {
          actionId: expect.stringMatching(UUID),
          supplementId: VITAMIN_D,
          occurredAt: NOW - 5 * MINUTE,
          confirmedDuplicate: true,
        },
      },
    })
    expect(duplicate?.resend().action.payload.actionId).not.toBe(ACTION_ID)
  })

  it("finds nothing for another failure, or for no failure", () => {
    expect(duplicateOf(failedWith(new Offline()))).toBeNull()
    const replayed = new DoseConflict({ code: "ACTION_REPLAY_MISMATCH", current: dosedAt(NOW) })
    expect(duplicateOf(failedWith(replayed))).toBeNull()
    expect(duplicateOf(null)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe("duplicateMessage", () => {
  it("says when the day's dose was logged and by whom, then asks", () => {
    expect(duplicateMessage("Vitamin D", dosedAt(NOW - 200 * MINUTE), HOUSEHOLD)).toBe(
      "Vitamin D was logged at 6:10 AM by Emily. Record another dose anyway?",
    )
  })
})
````

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import type { Sending } from "../src/sheets/sending.ts"
import { CLOSED, savedState } from "../src/sheets/sheets-state.ts"
import { restoredToast, TOAST_MS } from "../src/sheets/undo.ts"
import { dosedAt, NOW, VITAMIN_D } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const ACTION_ID = "4b1c2a5e-8f3d-4c6b-9a7e-2d1f0e3c5b6a"
const UUID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const DOSE = dosedAt(NOW - 60_000, { id: "dose-1", version: 1 })

const LOGGED: Sending = {
  sheet: "dose",
  action: {
    kind: "logDose",
    payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, supplementId: VITAMIN_D, occurredAt: NOW - 60_000, confirmedDuplicate: false },
  },
}

const DELETED: Sending = {
  sheet: "editDose",
  action: { kind: "deleteDose", id: "dose-1", payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1 } },
}

describe("a saved dose's toast", () => {
  it("says the dose was saved, with an Undo that deletes it at the version the household answered", () => {
    expect(savedState(CLOSED, LOGGED, { dose: DOSE, changed: [] }, NOW).toast).toEqual({
      message: "Dose saved",
      undo: { sheet: "undo", action: { kind: "deleteDose", id: "dose-1", payload: { actionId: UUID, version: 1 } } },
      until: NOW + TOAST_MS,
    })
  })

  it("says a dose was deleted, with an Undo that restores it", () => {
    const deleted = { ...DOSE, version: 2, deletedAt: NOW }
    expect(savedState(CLOSED, DELETED, { dose: deleted, changed: [] }, NOW).toast).toMatchObject({
      message: "Dose deleted",
      undo: { action: { kind: "restoreDose", id: "dose-1", payload: { version: 2 } } },
    })
  })

  it("says a dose was restored from Recently deleted, with an Undo that deletes it again", () => {
    expect(restoredToast({ dose: { ...DOSE, version: 3 }, changed: [] }, NOW, ACTION_ID)).toEqual({
      message: "Dose restored",
      undo: {
        sheet: "undo",
        action: { kind: "deleteDose", id: "dose-1", payload: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 3 } },
      },
      until: NOW + TOAST_MS,
    })
  })
})
````

````ts
import type { Dose, Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import { isPending } from "../../api/pending.ts"
import { clockTime } from "../../clock-time.ts"
import type { Display } from "./display.ts"

export interface SupplementRow {
  readonly id: string
  readonly name: string
  readonly title: string
  readonly detail: ReadonlyArray<string>
}

const present = (parts: ReadonlyArray<string>) => parts.filter((part) => part !== "")

const timesOf = (count: number) => (count === 2 ? "twice" : `${count} times`)

const whoLogged = ({ caregivers }: Display, id: string) => {
  const name = caregivers.find((caregiver) => caregiver.id === id)?.displayName
  return name === undefined ? "" : `by ${name}`
}

const rowOf = (supplement: Supplement, given: ReadonlyArray<Dose>, display: Display): SupplementRow => {
  const { id, name } = supplement
  const last = given.at(-1)
  if (last === undefined) return { id, name, title: `${name} not logged yet today`, detail: present([supplement.dose]) }
  const time = clockTime(last.occurredAt, display.timeZone, display.locale)
  const title =
    given.length === 1 ? `${name} logged at ${time}` : `${name} logged ${timesOf(given.length)} today, last at ${time}`
  const by = isPending(last.id) ? "Saving…" : whoLogged(display, last.createdBy)
  return { id, name, title, detail: present([by, last.dose]) }
}

export const supplementRows = (display: Display, doses: ReadonlyArray<Dose>): ReadonlyArray<SupplementRow> =>
  display.supplements
    .filter((supplement) => supplement.stoppedAt === null)
    .map((supplement) =>
      rowOf(
        supplement,
        doses.filter((dose) => dose.supplementId === supplement.id),
        display,
      ),
    )
````

````tsx
import { ChevronRightIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { Card, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "../../components/ui/card.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { KIND_ICONS } from "./kind-icons.ts"
import { Parts } from "./parts.tsx"
import type { SupplementRow } from "./supplement-rows.ts"

const Icon = KIND_ICONS.dose

const SupplementItem = ({ row }: { readonly row: SupplementRow }) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  return (
    <li>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-muted/50 active:bg-muted"
        onClick={() => sheets.show("dose", row.id)}
      >
        <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-muted">
          <Icon aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
        </span>
        <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
          <span className="font-medium">{row.title}</span>
          {row.detail.length === 0 ? null : (
            <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <Parts parts={row.detail} />
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
        <ChevronRightIcon aria-hidden="true" className="size-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />
      </button>
    </li>
  )
}

export const SupplementsCard = ({ rows }: { readonly rows: ReadonlyArray<SupplementRow> }) =>
  rows.length === 0 ? null : (
    <Card className="gap-0 py-0">
      <CardHeader className="py-3">
        <CardTitle id="supplements-title" className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Supplements
        </CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <ul aria-labelledby="supplements-title" className="divide-y border-t">
        {rows.map((row) => (
          <SupplementItem key={row.id} row={row} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </Card>
  )
````

- [ ] **Step 4: The dose sheet, and the second-dose question.**

````ts
import { type Dose, DoseConflict } from "@bt/contracts"
import { type Household, nameOf, timeOf } from "../../sheets/failure.ts"
import type { Failed, Sending } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"
import { NOW, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"

export interface DoseSession {
  readonly actionId: string
  readonly supplementId: string | null
  readonly when: When
  readonly confirmedDuplicate: boolean
}

const loggedIn = (failed: Failed | null) => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  return action?.kind === "logDose" ? action : null
}

export const supplementIdOf = (failed: Failed | null, editing: string | null) =>
  loggedIn(failed)?.payload.supplementId ?? editing

export const doseSessionOf = (
  failed: Failed | null,
  editing: string | null,
  zone: string,
  at: number | null,
): DoseSession => {
  const action = loggedIn(failed)
  if (action === null) {
    const when = at === null ? NOW : whenAt(at, zone)
    return { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), supplementId: editing, when, confirmedDuplicate: false }
  }
  const { actionId, supplementId, occurredAt, confirmedDuplicate } = action.payload
  return { actionId, supplementId, when: whenAt(occurredAt, zone), confirmedDuplicate }
}

export interface Duplicate {
  readonly failed: Failed
  readonly current: Dose
  readonly resend: () => Sending
}

export const duplicateOf = (failed: Failed | null): Duplicate | null => {
  const action = loggedIn(failed)
  if (failed === null || action === null) return null
  const { error } = failed
  if (!(error instanceof DoseConflict) || error.code !== "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY") return null
  const resend = (): Sending => ({
    sheet: failed.sending.sheet,
    action: { ...action, payload: { ...action.payload, actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), confirmedDuplicate: true } },
  })
  return { failed, current: error.current, resend }
}

export const duplicateMessage = (name: string, current: Dose, household: Household) =>
  `${name} was logged at ${timeOf(household, current.occurredAt)} by ${nameOf(household, current.createdBy)}. Record another dose anyway?`
````

````tsx
import type { Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { FailureNote } from "../../sheets/failure-note.tsx"
import type { Household } from "../../sheets/failure.ts"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import { resolveWhen } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import { doseSessionOf, duplicateOf } from "./dose-session.ts"
import { DuplicatePrompt } from "./duplicate-prompt.tsx"

interface DoseFormProps {
  readonly household: Household
  readonly supplement: Supplement
}

export const DoseForm = ({ household, supplement }: DoseFormProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const zone = household.timeZone
  const failed = failedFor(sheets, "dose")
  const [session] = useState(() => doseSessionOf(failed, sheets.editing, zone, sheets.at))
  const [when, setWhen] = useState(session.when)
  const now = useNow()
  const duplicate = duplicateOf(failed)
  if (duplicate !== null) return <DuplicatePrompt duplicate={duplicate} name={supplement.name} household={household} />
  const log = () => {
    if (sheets.open !== "dose") return
    const resolved = resolveWhen(when, zone, tapTime())
    if (!resolved.ok) return
    const { actionId, confirmedDuplicate } = session
    const payload = { actionId, supplementId: supplement.id, occurredAt: resolved.instant, confirmedDuplicate }
    sheets.send({ sheet: "dose", action: { kind: "logDose", payload } })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <WhenField label="When" when={when} onChange={setWhen} zone={zone} now={now} />
      <Button size="lg" className="h-16 text-lg" disabled={!resolveWhen(when, zone, now).ok} onClick={log}>
        Log {supplement.name}
      </Button>
      <FailureNote sheet="dose" />
    </>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import type { Household } from "../../sheets/failure.ts"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { type Duplicate, duplicateMessage } from "./dose-session.ts"

interface DuplicatePromptProps {
  readonly duplicate: Duplicate
  readonly name: string
  readonly household: Household
}

export const DuplicatePrompt = ({ duplicate, name, household }: DuplicatePromptProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const recordAnyway = () => {
    sheets.discard(duplicate.failed)
    sheets.send(duplicate.resend())
  }
  const dontRecord = () => {
    sheets.discard(duplicate.failed)
    sheets.close()
  }
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <p className="text-base">{duplicateMessage(name, duplicate.current, household)}</p>
      <Button size="lg" onClick={recordAnyway}>
        Record another dose
      </Button>
      <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" onClick={dontRecord}>
        Don't record it
      </Button>
    </div>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import { useMe } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Sheet } from "../../sheets/sheet.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { useHousehold } from "../../sheets/use-household.ts"
import { DoseForm } from "./dose-form.tsx"
import { supplementIdOf } from "./dose-session.ts"

export const DoseSheet = () => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const household = useHousehold()
  const { data: me } = useMe()
  const id = supplementIdOf(failedFor(sheets, "dose"), sheets.editing)
  const supplement = me?.household.supplements.find((one) => one.id === id)
  return (
    <Sheet sheet="dose" title={supplement?.name ?? "Supplement"} description="One tap saves it.">
      {household === null || supplement === undefined ? null : (
        <DoseForm household={household} supplement={supplement} />
      )}
    </Sheet>
  )
}
````

The tests below cover:
- AC 2's two taps
- the second-dose question, both ways
- a dose kept through a reload with no connection
- a picked time
- AC 6's two contexts

AC 6's test runs on the real Worker. It adds a "Test drops …" supplement with a random suffix, and stops it afterwards, so a rerun starts clean.

````ts
import { devices, expect, type Locator, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import {
  caregiversOf,
  type DoseAnswer,
  loseSaves,
  MINUTE,
  muteLiveUpdates,
  NOW,
  openToday,
  playHousehold,
  type Sent,
  todayWith,
} from "./played-household.ts"

const ACTION_ID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)

const tapper = () => {
  let taps = 0
  const tap = async (target: Locator) => {
    taps += 1
    await target.click()
  }
  return { tap, taps: () => taps }
}

const supplements = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })

const rowOf = (page: Page, name: string) => supplements(page).getByRole("button", { name: new RegExp(`^${name}`, "u") })

const doseSheet = (page: Page, name: string) => page.getByRole("dialog", { name })

interface Supplement {
  readonly id: string
  readonly name: string
}

const doseAt = (supplementId: string, occurredAt: number, by: string, id: string): DoseAnswer => ({
  id,
  supplementId,
  occurredAt,
  localDate: "2026-10-09",
  dose: "400 IU",
  version: 1,
  createdBy: by,
  createdAt: occurredAt,
  updatedBy: by,
  updatedAt: occurredAt,
  deletedAt: null,
})

const playDoses = async (page: Page, already: (vitaminD: string, emily: string) => DoseAnswer[] = () => []) => {
  const { andrew, emily, me } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const vitaminD: Supplement = me.household.supplements.find(({ name }: Supplement) => name === "Vitamin D")
  const doses = already(vitaminD.id, emily.id)
  const household = await playHousehold(page, { ...todayWith(), doses })
  await household.answer("**/api/doses", ({ body }: Sent) => {
    const { today } = household.played
    const sameDay = today.doses.filter(({ supplementId }) => supplementId === body.supplementId)
    const current = sameDay.at(-1)
    if (body.confirmedDuplicate !== true && current !== undefined) {
      return { status: 409, json: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "SUPPLEMENT_DUPLICATE_DAY", current } }
    }
    const dose = doseAt(body.supplementId ?? "", body.occurredAt ?? NOW, andrew.id, `dose-${today.doses.length + 1}`)
    household.played.today = { ...today, doses: [...today.doses, dose], recent: [{ kind: "dose", dose }] }
    return { status: 201, json: { dose, changed: [["today"], ["last24h"]] } }
  })
  return { played: household.played, vitaminD }
}

test.describe("Today's supplements, with the household's answers played by the test", () => {
  test("logs today's Vitamin D in two taps from its row, and says who logged it and when (AC 2)", async ({ page }) => {
    const { played, vitaminD } = await playDoses(page)
    await openToday(page)
    await expect(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D")).toHaveText(/Vitamin D not logged yet today\s*400 IU/u)
    const { tap, taps } = tapper()
    await tap(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D"))
    await tap(doseSheet(page, "Vitamin D").getByRole("button", { name: "Log Vitamin D" }))
    expect(taps()).toBe(2)
    await expect
      .poll(() => played.sent)
      .toEqual([
        {
          method: "POST",
          path: "/api/doses",
          body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, supplementId: vitaminD.id, occurredAt: NOW, confirmedDuplicate: false },
        },
      ])
    await expect(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D")).toHaveText(/Vitamin D logged at 9:30\sAM\s*by Andrew \(local\)/u)
    await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Last 24 hours" })).toContainText("Vitamin D")
    await expect(page.getByRole("status").filter({ hasText: "Dose saved" })).toBeVisible()
  })

  test("asks before a second dose that day, and records it once the parent means it", async ({ page }) => {
    const { played } = await playDoses(page, (vitaminD, emily) => [
      doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - 200 * MINUTE, emily, "dose-0"),
    ])
    await openToday(page)
    await expect(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D")).toHaveText(/Vitamin D logged at 6:10\sAM\s*by Emily \(local\)/u)
    await rowOf(page, "Vitamin D").click()
    const sheet = doseSheet(page, "Vitamin D")
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Log Vitamin D" }).click()
    await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toHaveText(
      /Vitamin D was logged at 6:10\sAM by Emily \(local\)\. Record another dose anyway\?/u,
    )
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Record another dose" }).click()
    await expect
      .poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => [body.actionId, body.confirmedDuplicate]))
      .toEqual([
        [ACTION_ID, false],
        [ACTION_ID, true],
      ])
    expect(new Set(played.sent.map(({ body }) => body.actionId)).size).toBe(2)
    await expect(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D")).toHaveText(/Vitamin D logged twice today, last at 9:30\sAM/u)
  })

  test("logs nothing more when the parent doesn't mean a second dose", async ({ page }) => {
    const { played } = await playDoses(page, (vitaminD, emily) => [
      doseAt(vitaminD, NOW - 200 * MINUTE, emily, "dose-0"),
    ])
    await openToday(page)
    await rowOf(page, "Vitamin D").click()
    const sheet = doseSheet(page, "Vitamin D")
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Log Vitamin D" }).click()
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Don't record it" }).click()
    await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
    expect(played.sent).toHaveLength(1)
    await expect(rowOf(page, "Vitamin D")).toHaveText(/Vitamin D logged at 6:10\sAM/u)
  })

  test("keeps a dose that failed with no connection through a reload, and asks before saving it under its actionId", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    const { andrew, me } = await caregiversOf(page)
    const vitaminD: Supplement = me.household.supplements.find(({ name }: Supplement) => name === "Vitamin D")
    await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
    const sent = await loseSaves(page, "**/api/doses", {
      lost: 1,
      until: Promise.resolve(),
      answer: ({ occurredAt }) => ({ dose: doseAt(vitaminD.id, occurredAt ?? NOW, andrew.id, "dose-1"), changed: [] }),
    })
    await muteLiveUpdates(page)
    await openToday(page)
    await rowOf(page, "Vitamin D").click()
    const sheet = doseSheet(page, "Vitamin D")
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Log Vitamin D" }).click()
    await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toContainText("No connection. This is kept on this phone:")
    await page.reload()
    await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toHaveText(/^This wasn't saved, Fri 9:30\sAM\. Save it now\?Save now$/u)
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save now" }).click()
    await expect(page.getByText("Dose saved")).toBeVisible()
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(sent[1]).toEqual(sent[0])
  })

  test("logs a dose at the time picked, read on the household's clock", async ({ page }) => {
    const { played } = await playDoses(page)
    await openToday(page)
    await rowOf(page, "Vitamin D").click()
    const sheet = doseSheet(page, "Vitamin D")
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Change" }).click()
    await sheet.getByLabel("When").fill("2026-10-09T08:50")
    await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Log Vitamin D" }).click()
    await expect.poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => body.occurredAt)).toEqual([NOW - 40 * MINUTE])
  })
})

test("shows a dose logged on one phone on the other within 2 seconds (AC 6)", async ({ browser, page }, testInfo) => {
  const name = `Test drops ${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`
  const added = await page.request.post("/api/supplements", {
    data: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), name, dose: "1 mL" },
  })
  expect(added.status()).toBe(201)
  const { supplement } = await added.json()
  const baseURL = testInfo.project.use.baseURL ?? ""
  const other = await browser.newContext({
    ...devices["iPhone 15"],
    baseURL,
    serviceWorkers: "block",
    storageState: {
      cookies: [],
      origins: [{ origin: baseURL, localStorage: [{ name: "bt.install-card", value: "dismissed" }] }],
    },
  })
  try {
    const watching = await other.newPage()
    await watching.goto("/")
    await expect(rowOf(watching, name)).toHaveText(new RegExp(`${name} not logged yet today`, "u"))
    await page.goto("/")
    await rowOf(page, name).click()
    await doseSheet(page, name)
      .getByRole("button", { name: `Log ${name}` })
      .click()
    await expect(rowOf(page, name)).toHaveText(new RegExp(`${name} logged at`, "u"))
    await expect(rowOf(watching, name)).toHaveText(new RegExp(`${name} logged at`, "u"), { timeout: 2_000 })
  } finally {
    await other.close()
    await page.request.patch(`/api/supplements/${supplement.id}`, {
      data: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version: 1, name, dose: "1 mL", stopped: true },
    })
  }
})
````

### Task 8: A dose's edit sheet

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/edit/dose-edit.ts`, `edit-dose-form.tsx`, `edit-dose-sheet.tsx`, `apps/web/test/dose-edit.test.ts`, `e2e/edit-dose.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/edit/edit-failure.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/today/recent-rows.ts`, `apps/web/src/shell/log-sheets.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/test/today-rows.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's swaps, `staleOf` and `editSheetOf`; M3.4's `Target`, `EditFailure`, `DeleteEntry`, `useCachedEntry` and `sameWhen`.
- Produces:
  - `DoseEditSession`: `{ actionId, target: Target, occurredAt, when }`
  - `doseEditSession(failed, dose, zone)`
  - `movedTo(session, when, zone, now): Resolved`, which keeps the exact time while When is left alone
  - `doseEdit(session, occurredAt): DoseAction` and `doseDelete(target): DoseAction`
  - `givenParts(display, dose)`
  - `EditDoseSheet`, titled "Edit dose"
  - `RowSheet` gains `"editDose"`. A recent dose row opens it unless the dose is still saving.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import { doseDelete, doseEdit, doseEditSession, givenParts, movedTo } from "../src/features/edit/dose-edit.ts"
import type { Failed } from "../src/sheets/sending.ts"
import { whenAt } from "../src/sheets/when.ts"
import { DISPLAY } from "./history-fixtures.ts"
import { dosedAt, MINUTE, NOW, supplementOf, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/u
const TO_THE_SECOND = NOW - 30 * MINUTE + 37_412
const DOSE = dosedAt(TO_THE_SECOND, { id: "dose-1", version: 2 })

describe("doseEditSession", () => {
  it("opens on the dose as it is, at its exact time", () => {
    expect(doseEditSession(null, DOSE, ZONE)).toEqual({
      actionId: expect.stringMatching(UUID),
      target: { id: "dose-1", version: 2 },
      occurredAt: TO_THE_SECOND,
      when: whenAt(TO_THE_SECOND, ZONE),
    })
  })

  it("reopens on an edit that wasn't saved, with its own action, version and time", () => {
    const failed: Failed = {
      sending: {
        sheet: "editDose",
        action: { kind: "editDose", id: "dose-1", payload: { actionId: "action-1", version: 1, occurredAt: NOW } },
      },
      error: new Offline(),
    }
    expect(doseEditSession(failed, DOSE, ZONE)).toEqual({
      actionId: "action-1",
      target: { id: "dose-1", version: 1 },
      occurredAt: NOW,
      when: whenAt(NOW, ZONE),
    })
  })
})

describe("a dose's edit", () => {
  const session = doseEditSession(null, DOSE, ZONE)

  it("keeps the exact time, seconds included, when the parent leaves When alone", () => {
    expect(movedTo(session, session.when, ZONE, NOW)).toEqual({ ok: true, instant: TO_THE_SECOND })
  })

  it("moves the dose to the minute picked, from the version it opened on", () => {
    const picked = whenAt(NOW - 60 * MINUTE, ZONE)
    const moved = movedTo(session, picked, ZONE, NOW)
    expect(moved).toEqual({ ok: true, instant: NOW - 60 * MINUTE })
    expect(doseEdit(session, NOW - 60 * MINUTE)).toEqual({
      kind: "editDose",
      id: "dose-1",
      payload: { actionId: session.actionId, version: 2, occurredAt: NOW - 60 * MINUTE },
    })
  })

  it("names the dose by its supplement and the dose it was given at, above the form", () => {
    const display = { ...DISPLAY, supplements: [supplementOf()] }
    expect(givenParts(display, DOSE)).toEqual(["Vitamin D", "400 IU"])
    expect(givenParts(display, { ...DOSE, dose: "" })).toEqual(["Vitamin D"])
  })

  it("deletes the dose from the version it opened on, under an action of its own", () => {
    expect(doseDelete(session.target)).toEqual({
      kind: "deleteDose",
      id: "dose-1",
      payload: { actionId: expect.stringMatching(UUID), version: 2 },
    })
  })
})
````

````ts
import type { Dose } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { DoseAction } from "../../api/actions.ts"
import type { Failed } from "../../sheets/sending.ts"
import { type Resolved, resolveWhen, sameWhen, type When, whenAt } from "../../sheets/when.ts"
import { type Display, supplementName } from "../today/display.ts"
import type { Target } from "./diaper-edit.ts"

export interface DoseEditSession {
  readonly actionId: string
  readonly target: Target
  readonly occurredAt: number
  readonly when: When
}

export const doseEditSession = (failed: Failed | null, dose: Dose, zone: string): DoseEditSession => {
  const action = failed?.sending.action
  if (action?.kind === "editDose") {
    const { actionId, version, occurredAt } = action.payload
    return { actionId, target: { id: action.id, version }, occurredAt, when: whenAt(occurredAt, zone) }
  }
  const { id, version, occurredAt } = dose
  return { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), target: { id, version }, occurredAt, when: whenAt(occurredAt, zone) }
}

export const movedTo = (session: DoseEditSession, when: When, zone: string, now: number): Resolved =>
  sameWhen(when, session.when) ? { ok: true, instant: session.occurredAt } : resolveWhen(when, zone, now)

export const doseEdit = (session: DoseEditSession, occurredAt: number): DoseAction => ({
  kind: "editDose",
  id: session.target.id,
  payload: { actionId: session.actionId, version: session.target.version, occurredAt },
})

export const givenParts = (display: Display, dose: Dose) =>
  [supplementName(display, dose.supplementId), dose.dose].filter((part) => part !== "")

export const doseDelete = ({ id, version }: Target): DoseAction => ({
  kind: "deleteDose",
  id,
  payload: { actionId: crypto.randomUUID(), version },
})
````

````tsx
import type { Dose, RecentEntry } from "@bt/contracts"
import { useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { DeleteEntry } from "../../sheets/delete-entry.tsx"
import { failedFor, useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { tapTime } from "../../sheets/tap-time.ts"
import { WhenField } from "../../sheets/when-field.tsx"
import { useNow } from "../../timer/use-now.ts"
import type { Display } from "../today/display.ts"
import { Parts } from "../today/parts.tsx"
import { doseDelete, doseEdit, doseEditSession, givenParts, movedTo } from "./dose-edit.ts"
import { EditFailure } from "./edit-failure.tsx"

interface EditDoseFormProps {
  readonly dose: Dose
  readonly display: Display
  readonly onUseTheirs: (current: RecentEntry) => void
}

export const EditDoseForm = ({ dose, display, onUseTheirs }: EditDoseFormProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const zone = display.timeZone
  const [session] = useState(() => doseEditSession(failedFor(sheets, "editDose"), dose, zone))
  const [when, setWhen] = useState(session.when)
  const now = useNow()
  const save = () => {
    if (sheets.open !== "editDose") return
    const moved = movedTo(session, when, zone, tapTime())
    if (moved.ok) sheets.send({ sheet: "editDose", action: doseEdit(session, moved.instant) })
  }
  const remove = () => {
    if (sheets.open === "editDose") sheets.send({ sheet: "editDose", action: doseDelete(session.target) })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <EditFailure sheet="editDose" display={display} onUseTheirs={onUseTheirs} />
      <p className="text-base font-medium">
        <Parts parts={givenParts(display, dose)} />
      </p>
      <WhenField label="When" when={when} onChange={setWhen} zone={zone} now={now} />
      <Button size="lg" className="h-14 text-lg" disabled={!movedTo(session, when, zone, now).ok} onClick={save}>
        Save
      </Button>
      <DeleteEntry noun="dose" emptied={null} onDelete={remove} />
    </>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMe } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Sheet } from "../../sheets/sheet.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { displayOf } from "../today/display.ts"
import { EditDoseForm } from "./edit-dose-form.tsx"
import { useCachedEntry } from "./use-cached-entry.ts"

const EditDoseContent = () => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const { data: me } = useMe()
  const cached = useCachedEntry(sheets.editing)
  const [shown, setShown] = useState({ entry: cached, taken: 0 })
  if (me === undefined) return null
  if (shown.entry?.kind !== "dose") return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">This dose was deleted.</p>
  return (
    <EditDoseForm
      key={shown.taken}
      dose={shown.entry.dose}
      display={displayOf(me.household)}
      onUseTheirs={(entry) => setShown(({ taken }) => ({ entry, taken: taken + 1 }))}
    />
  )
}

export const EditDoseSheet = () => (
  <Sheet sheet="editDose" title="Edit dose">
    <EditDoseContent />
  </Sheet>
)
````

Expected: PASS.

````ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import {
  caregiversOf,
  type DoseAnswer,
  MINUTE,
  NOW,
  openToday,
  playHousehold,
  savedBy,
  todayWith,
} from "./played-household.ts"

test.use({ timezoneId: "Europe/London" })

const ACTION_ID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const recent = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("list", { name: "Last 24 hours" })
const editSheet = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit dose" })

const playDose = async (page: Page, occurredAt = NOW - 30 * MINUTE) => {
  const { andrew, emily, me } = await caregiversOf(page)
  const vitaminD = me.household.supplements.find(({ name }: { name: string }) => name === "Vitamin D")
  const dose: DoseAnswer = {
    id: "dose-1",
    supplementId: vitaminD.id,
    occurredAt,
    localDate: "2026-10-09",
    dose: "400 IU",
    ...savedBy(andrew.id),
  }
  const showing = (shown: DoseAnswer | null) => {
    household.played.today = {
      ...todayWith(),
      recent: shown === null ? [] : [{ kind: "dose", dose: shown }],
      doses: shown === null ? [] : [shown],
    }
  }
  const household = await playHousehold(page, todayWith())
  showing(dose)
  return { ...household, dose, emily, showing }
}

const openTheDose = async (page: Page) => {
  await openToday(page)
  await recent(page)
    .getByRole("button", { name: /Vitamin D/u })
    .click()
  return editSheet(page)
}

test("moves a dose from Today, keeping its supplement and dose, and the card follows it", async ({ page }) => {
  const { answer, played, dose, showing } = await playDose(page)
  await answer("**/api/doses/dose-1", ({ body }) => {
    const moved = { ...dose, occurredAt: body.occurredAt ?? NOW, version: 2 }
    showing(moved)
    return { status: 200, json: { dose: moved, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  const sheet = await openTheDose(page)
  await expect(sheet).toContainText("Vitamin D · 400 IU")
  await expect(sheet.getByLabel("When")).toHaveValue("2026-10-09T09:00")
  await sheet.getByLabel("When").fill("2026-10-09T08:15")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent)
    .toEqual([
      {
        method: "PATCH",
        path: "/api/doses/dose-1",
        body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: 1, occurredAt: NOW - 75 * MINUTE },
      },
    ])
  await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })).toContainText("Vitamin D logged at 8:15")
})

test("keeps a dose's time to the second when it's saved untouched", async ({ page }) => {
  const occurredAt = NOW - 30 * MINUTE + 37_412
  const { answer, played, dose, showing } = await playDose(page, occurredAt)
  await answer("**/api/doses/dose-1", () => {
    showing({ ...dose, version: 2 })
    return { status: 200, json: { dose: { ...dose, version: 2 }, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  const sheet = await openTheDose(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect.poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => body.occurredAt)).toEqual([occurredAt])
})

test("deletes a dose after asking, then puts it back with Undo", async ({ page }) => {
  const { answer, played, dose, showing } = await playDose(page)
  await answer("**/api/doses/dose-1", () => {
    showing(null)
    return { status: 200, json: { dose: { ...dose, version: 2, deletedAt: NOW }, changed: [["today"], ["deleted"]] } }
  })
  await answer("**/api/doses/dose-1/restore", () => {
    showing({ ...dose, version: 3 })
    return { status: 200, json: { dose: { ...dose, version: 3 }, changed: [["today"], ["deleted"]] } }
  })
  const sheet = await openTheDose(page)
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete dose" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toContainText("Delete this dose?")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Delete", exact: true }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })).toContainText("Vitamin D not logged yet today")
  await page.getByRole("status").getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent.map(({ method, path, body }) => [method, path, body.version]))
    .toEqual([
      ["DELETE", "/api/doses/dose-1", 1],
      ["POST", "/api/doses/dose-1/restore", 2],
    ])
  await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })).toContainText("Vitamin D logged at 9:00")
})

test("says who changed the dose first, and saves mine on top of theirs when asked", async ({ page }) => {
  const { answer, played, dose, emily, showing } = await playDose(page)
  const theirs = { ...dose, occurredAt: NOW - 45 * MINUTE, version: 2, updatedBy: emily.id }
  await answer("**/api/doses/dose-1", ({ body }) => {
    if (body.version === 1) return { status: 409, json: { _tag: "Conflict", code: "STALE_VERSION", current: theirs } }
    const mine = { ...theirs, occurredAt: body.occurredAt ?? NOW, version: 3 }
    showing(mine)
    return { status: 200, json: { dose: mine, changed: [["today"]] } }
  })
  const sheet = await openTheDose(page)
  await sheet.getByLabel("When").fill("2026-10-09T08:15")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toContainText("Emily (local) changed this a moment ago.")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save mine anyway" }).click()
  await expect
    .poll(() => played.sent.map(({ body }) => [body.version, body.occurredAt]))
    .toEqual([
      [1, NOW - 75 * MINUTE],
      [2, NOW - 75 * MINUTE],
    ])
})
````

````ts
import { InvalidInput, SupplementConflict } from "@bt/contracts"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { Offline } from "../src/api/errors.ts"
import { saveFailure } from "../src/features/settings/messages.ts"
import {
  stopQuestion,
  supplementFieldMessage,
  supplementFieldsOf,
  supplementList,
} from "../src/features/settings/supplement-fields.ts"
import { supplementOf } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const VITAMIN_D = supplementOf()
const IRON = supplementOf({ id: "supplement-iron", name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" })
const PROBIOTIC = supplementOf({
  id: "supplement-probiotic",
  name: "Probiotic",
  dose: "",
  stoppedAt: Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5),
})

const formWith = (entries: Record<string, string>) => {
  const form = new FormData()
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(entries)) form.set(name, value)
  return form
}

describe("supplementList", () => {
  it("lists the supplements in use first, in the household's order, and the stopped ones apart, each with its dose", () => {
    expect(supplementList([VITAMIN_D, PROBIOTIC, IRON])).toEqual({
      inUse: [
        { id: VITAMIN_D.id, label: "Vitamin D · 400 IU", supplement: VITAMIN_D },
        { id: IRON.id, label: "Iron · 1 mL", supplement: IRON },
      ],
      stopped: [{ id: PROBIOTIC.id, label: "Probiotic", supplement: PROBIOTIC }],
    })
  })
})

describe("supplementFieldsOf", () => {
  it("reads the name and dose as typed, trimmed, with whether the supplement is given", () => {
    expect(
      supplementFieldsOf(formWith({ "supplement-name": "  Iron drops ", "supplement-dose": " 0.5 mL " }), false),
    ).toEqual({
      name: "Iron drops",
      dose: "0.5 mL",
      stopped: false,
    })
    expect(supplementFieldsOf(formWith({}), true)).toEqual({ name: "", dose: "", stopped: true })
  })
})

describe("supplementFieldMessage", () => {
  const refused = new InvalidInput({
    fieldErrors: [
      { path: "name", issue: "invalid" },
      { path: "dose", issue: "invalid" },
    ],
  })

  it("says what each refused field needs", () => {
    expect(supplementFieldMessage(refused, "name")).toBe("Enter a name of up to 60 characters.")
    expect(supplementFieldMessage(refused, "dose")).toBe("Keep the dose to 40 characters or fewer.")
  })

  it("says nothing for a field the household accepted, or for another failure", () => {
    const nameOnly = new InvalidInput({ fieldErrors: [{ path: "name", issue: "invalid" }] })
    expect(supplementFieldMessage(nameOnly, "dose")).toBeUndefined()
    expect(supplementFieldMessage(new Offline(), "name")).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

describe("stopQuestion", () => {
  it("asks before a supplement leaves Today, naming it and how it comes back", () => {
    expect(stopQuestion("Vitamin D")).toBe("Stop giving Vitamin D? Today won't list it until you give it again.")
  })
})

describe("a supplement's save failure", () => {
  it("says the other phone changed it when the household still refuses the save after putting it on top", () => {
    expect(saveFailure(new SupplementConflict({ code: "STALE_VERSION", current: VITAMIN_D }))).toBe(
      "This changed on another phone a moment ago. Check it and try again.",
    )
  })
})
````

- [ ] **Step 3: The list, the sheet and its form.**

````ts
import { InvalidInput, type Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { SupplementFields } from "../../api/supplements.ts"

export interface SupplementItem {
  readonly id: string
  readonly label: string
  readonly supplement: Supplement
}

const itemOf = (supplement: Supplement): SupplementItem => ({
  id: supplement.id,
  label: supplement.dose === "" ? supplement.name : `${supplement.name} · ${supplement.dose}`,
  supplement,
})

export const supplementList = (supplements: ReadonlyArray<Supplement>) => ({
  inUse: supplements.filter(({ stoppedAt }) => stoppedAt === null).map(itemOf),
  stopped: supplements.filter(({ stoppedAt }) => stoppedAt !== null).map(itemOf),
})

const text = (form: FormData, name: string) => String(form.get(name) ?? "").trim()

export const supplementFieldsOf = (form: FormData, stopped: boolean): SupplementFields => ({
  name: text(form, "supplement-name"),
  dose: text(form, "supplement-dose"),
  stopped,
})

export const stopQuestion = (name: string) => `Stop giving ${name}? Today won't list it until you give it again.`

const FIELD_MESSAGES = {
  name: "Enter a name of up to 60 characters.",
  dose: "Keep the dose to 40 characters or fewer.",
} as const

export const supplementFieldMessage = (error: Error | null, path: "name" | "dose"): string | undefined => {
  if (!(error instanceof InvalidInput)) return undefined
  return error.fieldErrors.some((fieldError) => fieldError.path === path) ? FIELD_MESSAGES[path] : undefined
}
````
