````tsx
import type { Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import type { FormEvent } from "react"
import { useSaveSupplement } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { SaveFailure } from "./save-failure.tsx"
import { StopSupplement } from "./stop-supplement.tsx"
import { supplementFieldMessage, supplementFieldsOf } from "./supplement-fields.ts"
import { TextField } from "./text-field.tsx"

export const SupplementForm = ({ shown }: { readonly shown: Supplement | null }) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const save = useSaveSupplement()
  const stopped = shown !== null && shown.stoppedAt !== null
  const send = (form: HTMLFormElement, stop: boolean) =>
    save.mutate({ shown, fields: supplementFieldsOf(new FormData(form), stop) }, { onSuccess: () => sheets.close() })
  const submit = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault()
    send(event.currentTarget, stopped)
  }
  return (
    <form noValidate className="flex flex-col gap-4" onSubmit={submit}>
      <TextField
        name="supplement-name"
        label="Name"
        defaultValue={shown?.name ?? ""}
        error={supplementFieldMessage(save.error, "name")}
      />
      <TextField
        name="supplement-dose"
        label="Dose"
        defaultValue={shown?.dose ?? ""}
        error={supplementFieldMessage(save.error, "dose")}
      />
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Doses already logged keep the dose they were given at.</p>
      <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={save.isPending}>
        {shown === null ? "Add supplement" : "Save"}
      </Button>
      {shown === null ? null : (
        <StopSupplement
          name={shown.name}
          stopped={stopped}
          disabled={save.isPending}
          onChange={(form) => send(form, !stopped)}
        />
      )}
      <SaveFailure error={save.error} />
    </form>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import { type MouseEvent, useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { stopQuestion } from "./supplement-fields.ts"

interface StopSupplementProps {
  readonly name: string
  readonly stopped: boolean
  readonly disabled: boolean
  readonly onChange: (form: HTMLFormElement) => void
}

const focusOnArrival = (button: HTMLButtonElement | null) => button?.focus()

export const StopSupplement = ({ name, stopped, disabled, onChange }: StopSupplementProps) => {
  const [asked, setAsked] = useState(false)
  const change = ({ currentTarget }: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (currentTarget.form !== null) onChange(currentTarget.form)
  }
  if (stopped || !asked) {
    return (
      <Button
        type="button"
        variant="outline"
        size="lg"
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={stopped ? change : () => setAsked(true)}
      >
        {stopped ? "Give it again" : "Stop giving it"}
      </Button>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border p-3">
      <p className="text-sm">{stopQuestion(name)}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button type="button" variant="destructive" size="lg" className="flex-1" disabled={disabled} onClick={change}>
          Stop
        </Button>
        <Button
          ref={focusOnArrival}
          type="button"
          variant="outline"
          size="lg"
          className="flex-1"
          onClick={() => setAsked(false)}
        >
          Keep giving it
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import { useMe } from "../../api/hooks.ts"
import { Sheet } from "../../sheets/sheet.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { SupplementForm } from "./supplement-form.tsx"

export const SupplementSheet = () => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const { data: me } = useMe()
  const shown = me?.household.supplements.find(({ id }) => id === sheets.editing) ?? null
  return (
    <Sheet sheet="supplement" title={shown === null ? "Add a supplement" : shown.name}>
      {me === undefined ? null : <SupplementForm shown={shown} />}
    </Sheet>
  )
}
````

````tsx
import type { Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"
import { ChevronRightIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { Button } from "../../components/ui/button.tsx"
import { useSheets } from "../../sheets/sheets-context.ts"
import { SettingsSection } from "./settings-section.tsx"
import { type SupplementItem, supplementList } from "./supplement-fields.ts"

interface SupplementItemsProps {
  readonly labelledBy: string
  readonly items: ReadonlyArray<SupplementItem>
}

const SupplementItems = ({ labelledBy, items }: SupplementItemsProps) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  return (
    <ul aria-labelledby={labelledBy} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {items.map(({ id, label }) => (
        <li key={id}>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="lg"
            className="w-full justify-between"
            onClick={() => sheets.show("supplement", id)}
          >
            {label}
            <ChevronRightIcon aria-hidden="true" />
          </Button>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  )
}

export const SupplementsSection = ({ supplements }: { readonly supplements: ReadonlyArray<Supplement> }) => {
  const sheets = useSheets()
  const { inUse, stopped } = supplementList(supplements)
  return (
    <SettingsSection titleId="supplements-label" title="Supplements" hint="Today shows each one in use.">
      {inUse.length === 0 ? null : <SupplementItems labelledBy="supplements-label" items={inUse} />}
      <Button variant="secondary" size="lg" onClick={() => sheets.show("supplement")}>
        Add a supplement
      </Button>
      {stopped.length === 0 ? null : (
        <>
          <h3 id="stopped-supplements-label" className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
            Stopped
          </h3>
          <SupplementItems labelledBy="stopped-supplements-label" items={stopped} />
        </>
      )}
    </SettingsSection>
  )
}
````

Expected: PASS.

````ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"

const ACTION_ID = expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/u)
const AT = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 16, 30)

interface Supplement {
  readonly id: string
  readonly name: string
  readonly dose: string
  readonly stoppedAt: number | null
  readonly version: number
  readonly createdBy: string | null
  readonly createdAt: number
  readonly updatedBy: string | null
  readonly updatedAt: number
}

interface Body {
  readonly name: string
  readonly dose: string
  readonly version?: number
  readonly stopped?: boolean
}

const playSupplements = async (page: Page) => {
  const me = await (await page.request.get("/api/me")).json()
  const vitaminD: Supplement = me.household.supplements.find(({ name }: Supplement) => name === "Vitamin D")
  const sent: Array<{ readonly method: string; readonly path: string; readonly body: Body }> = []
  const played = { supplements: [vitaminD], sent }
  await page.route("**/api/me", (route) =>
    route.fulfill({ json: { ...me, household: { ...me.household, supplements: played.supplements } } }),
  )
  await page.route("**/api/supplements**", async (route) => {
    const request = route.request()
    const path = new URL(request.url()).pathname
    const body: Body = request.postDataJSON()
    played.sent.push({ method: request.method(), path, body })
    const id = path.split("/").at(-1) ?? ""
    const current = played.supplements.find((supplement) => supplement.id === id)
    const saved: Supplement =
      current === undefined
        ? { ...vitaminD, id: "supplement-new", name: body.name, dose: body.dose, version: 1, stoppedAt: null }
        : {
            ...current,
            name: body.name,
            dose: body.dose,
            stoppedAt: body.stopped === true ? (current.stoppedAt ?? AT) : null,
            version: current.version + 1,
          }
    played.supplements = [...played.supplements.filter(({ id: kept }) => kept !== saved.id), saved]
    await route.fulfill({ status: current === undefined ? 201 : 200, json: { supplement: saved, changed: [["me"]] } })
  })
  return { played, vitaminD }
}

const section = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })
const stoppedList = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("list", { name: "Stopped" })
const sheetOf = (page: Page, name: string) => page.getByRole("dialog", { name })

test("adds a supplement from Settings with its dose, and Today then lists it", async ({ page }) => {
  const { played } = await playSupplements(page)
  await page.goto("/settings")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add a supplement" }).click()
  const sheet = sheetOf(page, "Add a supplement")
  await sheet.getByLabel("Name").fill("Iron")
  await sheet.getByLabel("Dose").fill(" 1 mL ")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Add supplement" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  expect(played.sent).toEqual([
    { method: "POST", path: "/api/supplements", body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, name: "Iron", dose: "1 mL" } },
  ])
  await expect(section(page).getByRole("button")).toHaveText(["Vitamin D · 400 IU", "Iron · 1 mL"])
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Today" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })).toContainText("Iron not logged yet today")
})

test("renames a supplement and changes its dose from the version it opened on", async ({ page }) => {
  const { played, vitaminD } = await playSupplements(page)
  await page.goto("/settings")
  await section(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Vitamin D · 400 IU" }).click()
  const sheet = sheetOf(page, "Vitamin D")
  await expect(sheet.getByLabel("Dose")).toHaveValue("400 IU")
  await sheet.getByLabel("Name").fill("Vitamin D3")
  await sheet.getByLabel("Dose").fill("800 IU")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click()
  await expect(sheet).toBeHidden()
  expect(played.sent).toEqual([
    {
      method: "PATCH",
      path: `/api/supplements/${vitaminD.id}`,
      body: { actionId: ACTION_ID, version: vitaminD.version, name: "Vitamin D3", dose: "800 IU", stopped: false },
    },
  ])
  await expect(section(page)).toContainText("Vitamin D3 · 800 IU")
})

test("stops giving a supplement, which then leaves Today, and gives it again", async ({ page }) => {
  const { played } = await playSupplements(page)
  await page.goto("/settings")
  await section(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Vitamin D · 400 IU" }).click()
  const sheet = sheetOf(page, "Vitamin D")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop giving it" }).click()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toHaveText(
    "Stop giving Vitamin D? Today won't list it until you give it again.StopKeep giving it",
  )
  expect(played.sent).toEqual([])
  await sheet.getByRole("alert").getByRole("button", { name: "Stop" }).click()
  await expect(stoppedList(page).getByRole("button")).toHaveText(["Vitamin D · 400 IU"])
  await expect(section(page)).toBeHidden()
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Today" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText("Totals")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByRole("list", { name: "Supplements" })).toBeHidden()
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: "Settings" }).click()
  await stoppedList(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Vitamin D · 400 IU" }).click()
  await sheetOf(page, "Vitamin D").getByRole("button", { name: "Give it again" }).click()
  await expect(section(page).getByRole("button")).toHaveText(["Vitamin D · 400 IU"])
  expect(played.sent.map(({ body }) => body.stopped)).toEqual([true, false])
})

test("keeps giving a supplement when the parent changes their mind about stopping it", async ({ page }) => {
  const { played } = await playSupplements(page)
  await page.goto("/settings")
  await section(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Vitamin D · 400 IU" }).click()
  const sheet = sheetOf(page, "Vitamin D")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop giving it" }).click()
  const keep = sheet.getByRole("alert").getByRole("button", { name: "Keep giving it" })
  await expect(keep).toBeFocused()
  await keep.click()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("alert")).toBeHidden()
  await expect(sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Stop giving it" })).toBeVisible()
  expect(played.sent).toEqual([])
})

test("says what a name needs before sending a supplement with none", async ({ page }) => {
  const { played } = await playSupplements(page)
  await page.goto("/settings")
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add a supplement" }).click()
  const sheet = sheetOf(page, "Add a supplement")
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Add supplement" }).click()
  await expect(sheet.getByText("Enter a name of up to 60 characters.")).toBeVisible()
  expect(played.sent).toEqual([])
})
````

### Task 10: History's Day and Week views

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/test/history-doses.test.ts`, `e2e/history-doses.spec.ts`
- Modify: History's `history-kinds.ts`, `day-model.ts`, `week-model.ts`, `week-names.ts`, `week-marks.tsx`, `week-legend.tsx` and `kind-chips.tsx`; `e2e/played-day.ts` and `e2e/played-week.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/test/history-kinds.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `Entry`, markers and details; Task 5's `WeekPeriod.doses`.
- Produces:
  - `SHOWN_KINDS = ["feeds", "diapers", "growth", "supplements"]`, and `KIND_LABELS.supplements = "Supplements"`
  - `DaySlice.doses` and `DayPieces.doses`. `shownSlice` hides doses when Supplements is off.
  - `WeekColumn.doses: ReadonlyArray<PlacedCluster>` and `WeekColumn.dosesShown`. A day with a dose counts as logged.
  - a column's name adds "a dose" or "N doses"
  - the Week view's lanes: blocks `left-0.5 w-[46%]`, bottles `left-[50%] w-[15%]`, diapers `left-[66%] w-[15%]`, and doses `left-[82%] w-[15%]` (hollow dots: `border border-primary bg-card`), with a "Dose" legend entry
  - History's chips wrap
  - in `e2e/played-day.ts`: `dose(id, supplementId, at, by)` and a typed `DayAnswer.doses`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

````ts
import type { WeekPeriod } from "@bt/contracts"
import { isoWeekDates, zonedDayBounds } from "@bt/domain"
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest"
import { dayModel, entriesIn } from "../src/features/history/day-model.ts"
import { markersOf } from "../src/features/history/grid-markers.ts"
import { hiddenExcept, hiddenIn, hiddenNotice, hideOf } from "../src/features/history/history-kinds.ts"
import { weekModel } from "../src/features/history/week-model.ts"
import { columnName } from "../src/features/history/week-names.ts"
import { DATE, DISPLAY, dayWith, LOCALE, local, NOW_ON_DAY } from "./history-fixtures.ts"
import { dosedAt, NO_TOTALS, supplementOf, ZONE } from "./today-fixtures.ts"

const MORNING = dosedAt(local("08:00"), { id: "dose-morning" })
const EVENING = dosedAt(local("20:00"), { id: "dose-evening" })
const SHOWN = { ...DISPLAY, supplements: [supplementOf()] }
const DOSED_DAY = dayWith({ feeds: [], diapers: [], measurements: [], doses: [MORNING] })

const WEEK = "2026-W41"

const weekOf = (doses = [MORNING]): WeekPeriod => {
  const days = isoWeekDates(WEEK).map((date) => ({ date, bounds: zonedDayBounds(date, ZONE), totals: NO_TOTALS }))
  return {
    week: WEEK,
    bounds: { start: days[0]?.bounds.start ?? 0, end: days.at(-1)?.bounds.end ?? 0 },
    asOf: NOW_ON_DAY,
    totals: NO_TOTALS,
    days,
    feeds: [],
    diapers: [],
    sleeps: [],
    measurements: [],
    doses,
  }
}

const CONTEXT = { zone: ZONE, now: NOW_ON_DAY, today: DATE, birthDate: "2026-09-01", hidden: hiddenIn(undefined) }

const columnOn = (week: WeekPeriod, context = CONTEXT) => {
  const column = weekModel(week, context).find(({ date }) => date === DATE)
  if (column === undefined) throw new Error(`expected a column for ${DATE}`)
  return column
}

describe("the Supplements chip", () => {
  it("joins History's filters after Growth, read from and written to the address", () => {
    expect([...hiddenIn("supplements,diapers")]).toEqual(["supplements", "diapers"])
    expect(hideOf(new Set(["supplements", "feeds"]))).toBe("feeds,supplements")
    expect([...hiddenExcept(["feeds", "diapers", "growth"])]).toEqual(["supplements"])
    expect(hiddenNotice(hiddenIn("supplements"))).toBe("Supplements hidden")
  })
})

describe("a dose on the Day view", () => {
  it("is a marker at its time, named by its supplement, that opens its details", () => {
    const model = dayModel(DOSED_DAY, ZONE, NOW_ON_DAY)
    const [marker] = markersOf(model, SHOWN, NOW_ON_DAY)
    expect(marker).toMatchObject({ ids: [MORNING.id], kind: "dose", text: "Vitamin D" })
    expect(marker?.name).toMatch(/^Vitamin D at 8:00\sAM: 400 IU$/u)
    expect(entriesIn(model, [MORNING.id])).toEqual([{ kind: "dose", dose: MORNING }])
  })

  it("leaves the grid when Supplements is off, and is still found by id", () => {
    const model = dayModel(DOSED_DAY, ZONE, NOW_ON_DAY, hiddenIn("supplements"))
    expect(model.grid.clusters).toEqual([])
    expect(entriesIn(model, [MORNING.id])).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})

describe("a dose on the Week view", () => {
  it("is a dot in a lane of its own, at its time on the household's clock", () => {
    const column = columnOn(weekOf([MORNING, EVENING]))
    expect(column.doses.map(({ ids }) => ids)).toEqual([[MORNING.id], [EVENING.id]])
    expect(column.grid.clusters).toEqual([])
    expect(column.diapers).toEqual([])
  })

  it("names how many doses a day holds, and leaves them out when Supplements is off", () => {
    expect(columnName(columnOn(weekOf()), "ml", CONTEXT.hidden, LOCALE)).toBe("Fri, Oct 9: a dose")
    expect(columnName(columnOn(weekOf([MORNING, EVENING])), "ml", CONTEXT.hidden, LOCALE)).toBe("Fri, Oct 9: 2 doses")
    const hidden = hiddenIn("supplements")
    const column = columnOn(weekOf(), { ...CONTEXT, hidden })
    expect(column.doses).toEqual([])
    expect(columnName(column, "ml", hidden, LOCALE)).toBe("Fri, Oct 9: nothing shown")
  })
})
````

````diff
 }
````

````ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test"
import { DAY, dose, emptyDay, local, openHistory, playHistory } from "./played-day.ts"
import { playWeeks, THIS_WEEK, weekGrid, weekOf } from "./played-week.ts"

test.use({ timezoneId: "Europe/London" })

const playDosedDay = async (page: Page) => {
  const me = await (await page.request.get("/api/me")).json()
  const vitaminD = me.household.supplements.find(({ name }: { name: string }) => name === "Vitamin D")
  const { played, emily } = await playHistory(page, [])
  const day = { ...emptyDay(DAY), doses: [dose("dose-1", vitaminD.id, local("08:00"), emily)] }
  played.days.push(day)
  return { day }
}

const doseMarker = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("button", { name: /^Vitamin D at 8:00\sAM: 400 IU$/u })

test("shows a dose on the Day view at its time, opening its details and then its edit sheet", async ({ page }) => {
  await playDosedDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  await doseMarker(page).click()
  const details = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Vitamin D" })
  await expect(details).toContainText("400 IU")
  await expect(details).toContainText("Emily (local)")
  await details.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit" }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Edit dose" })).toContainText("Vitamin D · 400 IU")
})

test("hides doses with the Supplements chip, and says so", async ({ page }) => {
  await playDosedDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  await expect(doseMarker(page)).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole("group", { name: "Show" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Supplements" }).click()
  await expect(doseMarker(page)).toBeHidden()
  await expect(page.getByText("Supplements hidden")).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/hide=supplements/u)
})

test("fits the four chips on a 360-pixel-wide phone, wrapping them rather than scrolling the page", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 360, height: 640 })
  await playDosedDay(page)
  await openHistory(page)
  const chips = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Show" }).getByRole("button")
  await expect(chips).toHaveText(["Feeds", "Diapers", "Growth", "Supplements"])
  const edges = await chips.evaluateAll((nodes) =>
    nodes.map((node) => {
      const { left, right } = node.getBoundingClientRect()
      return [Math.round(left), Math.round(right)]
    }),
  )
  expect(edges.every(([left = 0, right = 0]) => left >= 0 && right <= 360)).toBe(true)
  expect(await page.locator("html").evaluate((html) => html.scrollWidth - html.clientWidth)).toBe(0)
})

test("shows a dose on the Week view in its own lane, named in its day's link and the legend", async ({ page }) => {
  const { day } = await playDosedDay(page)
  await playWeeks(page, { [THIS_WEEK]: weekOf(THIS_WEEK, [day]) })
  await openHistory(page, "?view=week")
  await expect(weekGrid(page).getByRole("link", { name: "Fri, Oct 9: a dose" })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText("Dose", { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
})
````

Run: `pnpm exec playwright test --config .superpowers/e2e.config.ts e2e/history-doses.spec.ts e2e/history.spec.ts e2e/history-grid.spec.ts e2e/history-week.spec.ts`
Expected: PASS on both engines. Then remove `className="flex-wrap"` from `kind-chips.tsx`, and expect "fits the four chips on a 360-pixel-wide phone…" to fail on both engines; put it back.

- [ ] **Step 3: The menu's supplements.**

````ts
import type { Supplement } from "@bt/contracts"

export interface SupplementChoice {
  readonly id: string
  readonly label: string
}

export const supplementChoices = (supplements: ReadonlyArray<Supplement>): ReadonlyArray<SupplementChoice> =>
  supplements.filter(({ stoppedAt }) => stoppedAt === null).map(({ id, name }) => ({ id, label: name }))
````

````diff
diff --git a/CLAUDE.md b/CLAUDE.md
index 69950eb..2fdbd6b 100644
--- a/CLAUDE.md
+++ b/CLAUDE.md
@@ -19,7 +19,7 @@ Work is tracked in GitHub issues. The Baby Tracker Roadmap artifact was frozen o
 
 - **Releases:** a merge to `main` deploys once CI passes on it (`deploy.yml`), so say in a PR's description that merging deploys it. `pnpm release` in the main checkout is the fallback, on Andrew's word only, and a Durable Object class change can only deploy by hand. Deploys, pausing them and recovering from a bad one are in `docs/runbooks/access.md`. After each deploy, Andrew opens the app online once on each phone, so its offline copy updates.
 - **Migrations:** they're forward-only. Before one merges, try it on a fresh `pnpm admin export` (rebuild the tables from the export's `migrations`, insert its rows with foreign keys off, then apply the new migration), run the restore drill in `docs/runbooks/restore.md`, and run `pnpm bench`. Run `pnpm bench` after any change to a period read, too.
-- **Receipts after a deploy:** every endpoint built on `mutations` (`apps/worker/src/mutations.ts`) stores its response as a receipt, which a retry within a day replays: today `Diaper`, `Feed`, `Measurement`, `Household` and `Child`. A field that's added, removed or renamed, or a tightened check, must stay decodable for 24 hours, or that retry answers 500. Only `Feed` has a test for it: `apps/worker/test/feeds.test.ts`'s "decode the response and changed keys stored before a deploy…".
+- **Receipts after a deploy:** every endpoint built on `mutations` (`apps/worker/src/mutations.ts`) stores its response as a receipt, which a retry within a day replays: today `Diaper`, `Feed`, `Measurement`, `Supplement`, `Dose`, `Household` and `Child`. A field that's added, removed or renamed, or a tightened check, must stay decodable for 24 hours, or that retry answers 500. Only `Feed` has a test for it: `apps/worker/test/feeds.test.ts`'s "decode the response and changed keys stored before a deploy…".
 - **Installed apps after a deploy:** an app older than the Worker reads `ChangedMessage`, so add fields to it, but never rename or remove one.
 - **25-hour days in tests:** tzdata 2026c keeps America/Vancouver on UTC−7 after 1 Nov 2026, so it has no 25-hour day that year; use America/Los_Angeles.
 - **Service workers in E2E:** `playwright.config.ts` blocks them, so `page.route` sees every request; only `offline.spec.ts` and `sheets-offline.spec.ts` allow them. Playwright's block resolves `register` rather than refusing it, so no E2E test sees a refused registration.
````

## Finish

- [ ] **Step 1: Merge `main` if it moved** (`git fetch && git merge origin/main`), and M3.1's branch head too until M3.1 is on `main`.
  - If M3.2 merged first, reconcile the places both touched, as the Handoffs below say. Then run every gate and the E2E suite.
  - A peer's new `Today`, period or `Deleted` literal needs `doses: []`, and a new `Me` literal needs `supplements: []`.
- [ ] **Step 2: The final review.** One fresh reviewer on Opus reads the whole branch against this plan. Brief them with the Review Focus and these questions:
  - At 9:10 Emily logs Vitamin D. Andrew's phone is offline, and he taps Log at 9:12, so his dose is held. When he's back online and taps Save now, what does he see?
  - A dose logged at 11:50 PM is moved to 12:10 AM. Which days change, does Today's card count it, and does the same-day question run?
  - Andrew renames Vitamin D and changes its dose while Emily's sheet is open. What does her button say, and what dose does her dose keep?
  - Can a time zone change, a move or a restore leave a dose under the wrong date? Can a dose be saved twice from one tap?
  - Does any screen count a dose as a feed or a diaper, or drop the doses from a cached day when a feed is edited?

  Fix its Critical and Important findings test first, plus the cheap Minors that touch data or tests (CodeRabbit raises those otherwise). Then re-review the fixes.
- [ ] **Step 3: The gates on the last commit.**
  - `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test` and `pnpm coverage`
  - `pnpm build`, `pnpm bundle:check` and `pnpm pins:check`
  - `pnpm api:docs`, with no change
  - `pnpm bench`
  - the E2E suite on port 4209
- [ ] **Step 4: The dry run on production's data (Andrew's condition for the merge, D5).**
  1. Wait until M3.1 has deployed, so the export holds 0007.
  2. From the worktree, run `node --env-file=<main checkout>/.admin-token.env scripts/admin.ts export ~/baby-tracker-before-0008.json`.
  3. Then run `pnpm migrations:dry-run ~/baby-tracker-before-0008.json`, and expect "Applied 0008-supplements on top of the export: …".

  Report only that summary line. The file holds her name and birth date, so it stays on the Mac and out of git. If the export already lists `0008-supplements`, something deployed it early: stop and ask Andrew.

Open an issue labelled `andrew` and assigned to him: "Run the restore drill before M3.3 merges", pointing at `docs/runbooks/restore.md` › The drill. Andrew chose a fresh drill for M3.3 in the second grill, not M3.1's.

  - If M3.1's PR hasn't merged yet, open it against M3.1's branch, so the diff is M3.3's alone. Say so at the top of the body, and retarget it to `main` once M3.1 lands. Either way it merges after M3.1 (D5).
  - CodeRabbit reviews only PRs into `main` by itself. On a PR into another branch, ask it with an `@coderabbitai review` comment.

  Its body says:
  - `Closes #45`
  - merging deploys migration 0008 once CI passes (M5.7). It isn't a Durable Object class change, so CI can deploy it.
  - the dry run's summary line, and when the export was taken
  - the restore point: each deploy records one, and `docs/runbooks/restore.md` rewinds to it
  - each phone picks up the deploy by itself (#61, merged as #81): it saves the new build the next time it's online, and opens it the next time the app comes back online with nothing open

    - log a dose from History's Log at menu

## Handoffs (not M3.3 work)

- **M3.2 (the sleep UI, 4e):** whichever of M3.2 and M3.3 merges second reconciles these:
  - `SHOWN_KINDS` becomes `["feeds", "diapers", "sleep", "growth", "supplements"]`. Keep `kind-chips.tsx`'s `flex-wrap`, since five chips don't fit any phone.
  - On the Week view, the sleep stripe goes at the left, at about 9 %. The bars take about 37 %, and the bottle, diaper and dose dot lanes about 15 % each (4e's figures).
  - `SheetKind`, `NEW_ENTRIES`, `TODAY_SHEETS`, `CLOSES_ON_SEND`, `RecentEntry`, `recentRow`, `Entry`, `markerText`, `entryName` and `list-rows.ts`' `startOf` and `nursedSpan` each gain both kinds.
  - `TimedSheet` gains both `"pastSleep"` and `"dose"`. The Log at menu keeps Sleep after Diaper, and the supplement buttons after Measurement.
  - Today's cards: the Supplements card stays under the feed card. M3.2's sleep line goes where its plan puts it.
- **M5.2 (accessibility):** Today's Supplements list is labelled by its card title, and the dose sheet, the edit sheet and Settings' sheet are new dialogs. Check them with VoiceOver.
